Tool result:

499	
500	## 11. `0A` direct mode: a real bug and a more-permissive read path
501	
502	`0A` with a first byte `< $08` enters "direct mode" (the OBD Mode 6 / data-stream path,
503	§1), which loads `cs_streamlist` (`$07A0`) and then streams it back via
504	`consult_reply_direct` (`$9CDD`). Two findings here; neither leads anywhere today, but
505	the second is the most promising thread to pull if anything else turns up later.
506	
507	- **Bug: integer underflow / missing lower bound on the value byte** (`$5239`–`$531F`).
508	  For the "high" sub-paths (value `≥ $80`) the table offset is `(value − $80 − 1) × 4`.
509	  The per-branch check only bounds the top (e.g. `CMP #$84` at `L_5298`), so **`value =
510	  $80` is accepted and underflows**: `0 − 1 = $FF`, `× 4 = $3FC`, low byte `$FC`. The
511	  handler then reads two stream descriptors from `table_base + $FC`, far past the 16-byte
512	  table. Triggers: `0A <fb> 80` for `fb ∈ {0,2,5,6,7}`. `fb=0` self-rejects (the OOB
513	  words at `$EA6B` are `0000`); `fb=2/5/6/7` stream two bytes from fixed unintended ROM
514	  addresses (`$90F9`,`$A902` / `$5809`,`$2889` / `$42F8`,`$FF29` / `$0926`,`$8958`). The
515	  intended minimum was `value = $81` (offset 0). **No new access** — those are bank-0 ROM
516	  bytes `C9` already returns — but it is a genuine input-validation bug. Read-only and
517	  safe to poke on the car (direct mode writes no outputs); good for confirming the
518	  analysis.
519	- **`consult_reply_direct` has no bounds check and no forwarding.** It does `LDA
520	  $07A0,X; TAX; LDA $00,X` (`$9CF1`): it dereferences each `cs_streamlist` entry as a
521	  16-bit address and reads it **locally in data bank 0, with none of the `$5D40–$623F`
522	  forward / range logic that `consult_reply_c9` applies** (§1). So this path is more
523	  permissive than `C9`: an entry pointing into `$5D40–$623F` would read *this* CPU's
524	  hidden code there directly. It doesn't matter in practice, though — every reachable
525	  `cs_streamlist` value comes from fixed ROM (the descriptor tables `$E95F–$EA80`, or the
526	  fixed out-of-bounds bytes above), and scanning all of those tables found **no** entry in
527	  `$5D40–$623F`; there's no command that sets `cs_streamlist` to anything else. But it
528	  reframes the question: it's no longer "can the `C9` range check be gotten around" (there's
529	  none here to get around) but "**is there any way to write a chosen 16-bit value into
530	  `$07A0`**". None found — the active-test value write stops at `$079C`, the `cs_argbuf`
531	  overflow reaches only `$08FA` — so this stays a dead end, but it's the most promising
532	  remaining direction.
533	- **Swept — that write does not exist.** Checked every store in the ROM that targets
534	  `$07A0–$07A3`: there are exactly three (`$5224`, `$5327`, `$5331`), and all write
535	  fixed data — a descriptor-table pointer, or two 16-bit words read from ROM at
536	  `table_base + offset` (even the underflow source `table_base + $FC` is ROM, never the
537	  `$08xx` argument buffer). The only indexed write that could be *redirected* onto `$07A0`
538	  is the at_values store (`$519D`, dest `$076F + at_slot`); `at_slot` would have to reach
539	  `$31` (49) and it is written only at `$515F`, from the descriptor, maxing at 45 — and
540	  nowhere else in the image, so it can't be left stale high either. **Conclusion:
541	  `cs_streamlist` is unconditionally fixed-content; `consult_reply_direct`'s missing hole
542	  check cannot be aimed from the Consult interface.** Lead closed.
543	- **Exhaustively enumerated the entries, too.** For completeness (since `0A` can be fired
544	  any number of times), every accepted direct-mode `(first-byte, value)` was enumerated —
545	  71 configurations across the five dispatches, each producing two `cs_streamlist` words
546	  from its fixed `table_base + offset` (including the `value=$80` underflow to `+$FC`).
547	  **None of the 142 resulting addresses falls in `$5D40–$623F`.** Every one is low RAM/IO
548	  (`$03xx–$08xx`), low ROM (`$23xx–$25xx`), or fixed garbage (`$90F9`,`$5809`,`$42F8`,
549	  `$8958`) — all bank-0 and already `C9`-readable. Nearest misses: `fb=5 v=$80` → `$5809`
550	  (just below the hole) and `fb=6 v=$80` → `$FF29` (inside `$FE00–$FFCF`, already readable
551	  with `$1207.5` clear). So the engine cannot be handed a hole address, and since it is a
552	  single dereference (`mem[entry]`, not `mem[mem[entry]]`), no RAM cell we can write turns
553	  into a second hop. The hidden code at `$5D40–$623F` stays off-limits to software.
554

Find n= if present\n        nm = re.search(r'n=(\\d+)', line)\n        n = nm.group(1) if nm else '?'\n        runs.append((i, target, n, line.strip()))\n\nfor i, (line_num, target, n, text) in enumerate(runs):\n    print(f'Run {i}: line {line_num+1}, {text}')\n\ndef find_c9_reads(start, end):\n    results = {}\n    i = start\n    while i < end:\n        line = lines[i].strip()\n        if 'TX c9' in line and 'TX f0' not in line:\n            m = re.findall(r'c9 ([0-9a-f]{2}) ([0-9a-f]{2})', line)\n            if m:\n                addrs = [int(h, 16) << 8 | int(l, 16) for h, l in m]\n                for j in range(i+1, min(i+50, end)):\n                    if 'TX f0' in lines[j]:\n                        for k in range(j+1, min(j+50, end)):\n                            if 'RX ff' in lines[k]:\n                                k += 1\n                                m2 = re.search(r'RX ([0-9a-f]{2})', lines[k].strip())\n                                if m2:\n                                    flen = int(m2.group(1), 16)\n                                    data = []\n                                    for n in range(flen):\n                                        k += 1\n                                        if k < end:\n                                            m3 = re.search(r'RX ([0-9a-f]{2})', lines[k].strip())\n                                            if m3:\n                                                data.append(int(m3.group(1), 16))\n                                    for idx, addr in enumerate(addrs):\n                                        if idx < len(data):\n                                            results[addr] = data[idx]\n                                break\n                        break\n        i += 1\n    return results\n\n# For each run, find before/after for the target address\nprint()\nfor ri, (run_start, target_hex, n, text) in enumerate(runs):\n    target_addr = int(target_hex, 16)\n    \n    # Find markers\n    before_line = after_line = close_line = None\n    for i in range(run_start, min(run_start + 3000, len(lines))):\n        if '## before' in lines[i] and before_line is None:\n            before_line = i\n        elif '## after' in lines[i] and after_line is None:\n            after_line = i\n        elif '## close' in lines[i]:\n            close_line = i\n            break\n    \n    if before_line and after_line and close_line:\n        before = find_c9_reads(run_start, before_line)\n        after = find_c9_reads(after_line, close_line)\n        \n        tb = before.get(target_addr)\n        ta = after.get(target_addr)\n        \n        flags_b = before.get(0x07E3)\n        flags_a = after.get(0x07E3)\n        sub_b = before.get(0x07E7)\n        sub_a = after.get(0x07E7)\n        \n        changed = tb != ta if tb is not None and ta is not None else None\n        \n        print(f'Run {ri} (n={n}): target \\${target_addr:04X}')\n        print(f'  target: before=\\${tb:02X}, after=\\${ta:02X}  {\\\"CHANGED\\\" if changed else \\\"unchanged\\\"}'\n              if tb is not None and ta is not None else f'  target: incomplete data')\n        print(f'  FLAGS:  before=\\${flags_b:02X}, after=\\${flags_a:02X}' if flags_b and flags_a else '')\n        print(f'  SUB_VAL: before=\\${sub_b:02X}, after=\\${sub_a:02X}' if sub_b is not None and sub_a is not None else '')\n        print()\n\" 2>&1",

}

405	  bytes unchecked, pushing `cs_argc` to 41 and writing one byte past the 40-byte
406	  `cs_argbuf` (`$08D2`). The reply loop then reads ~2 bytes beyond the buffer as an extra
407	  address — but still a bank-0 address read with data bank 0, i.e. memory `C9` already
408	  reaches. A latent off-by-one, not a way to read anything new. Chased to the end: the
409	  overflow byte lands at `$08FA`, which reset's RAM clear leaves untouched (`LDX #$08F9`
410	  down, `$3DAD`) and which **no code anywhere reads** — dead padding, so it corrupts
411	  nothing. And bank 1 can't be reached this way regardless: the data-bank register is set
412	  by `LDT #$00` only (`$3D80`, `$4486`) and never changed, and the ROM contains **no
413	  long-indirect (`[dp]`) read** — so every Consult-reachable read executes in data bank 0.

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""c9_overrun_test: verify the c9_find_hidden buffer overrun (ROM_ANALYSIS.md §10).
3	
4	Sends 16 C9 addresses all inside $5D40-$623F to trigger the out-of-bounds write
5	in c9_find_hidden ($543F), then reads back corruption signatures to confirm.
6	
7	The bug: c9_find_hidden has no upper-bound check on c9_hidden_n ($07E1). The
8	arrays c9_hidden_addr ($07C3, 2-byte stride) and c9_hidden_idx ($07D7, 1-byte)
9	hold 10 entries each, but one request can queue 20 addresses in the forwarded
10	range. At i=15 the 16-bit addr store lands on $07E1 itself, writing attacker-
11	controlled data (addr - $5000) into c9_hidden_n and the reply cursor $07E2.
12	The idx store then uses the corrupted n to pick a destination in $07D7-$08D6.
13	
14	SAFE: the overrun is confined to Consult/active-test state ($07C3-$08D6).
15	No RAM function pointer exists (ROM_ANALYSIS.md §10), so no code redirect.
16	Re-init does NOT clear it — only a key cycle does. Run with key on, engine off.
17	
18	Usage: python3 tools/c9_overrun_test.py [--dev /dev/ttyUSB0] [--dry-run]

21	import datetime
22	import os

27	ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
28	sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

30	from consult.protocol import Consult, ConsultError, ENGINE_IDS

33	LOG_DIR = os.path.join(ROOT, "logs")

35	# --- Constants from the disassembly ---
36	
37	HIDDEN_ADDR_BASE = 0x07C3      # c9_hidden_addr: 10 entries × 2 bytes
38	HIDDEN_IDX_BASE  = 0x07D7      # c9_hidden_idx:  10 entries × 1 byte
39	HIDDEN_N         = 0x07E1      # c9_hidden_n (count)
40	REPLY_CURSOR     = 0x07E2      # used by consult_reply_c9 ($9B3F)
41	FORWARD_LO       = 0x5D40      # C9 addresses in this range are forwarded
42	FORWARD_HI       = 0x623F
43	
44	# RAM regions to dump (contiguous ranges, read via C9 in 8-byte chunks).
45	# Each: (start, length, label).
46	DUMP_REGIONS = [
47	    (0x0740, 0x30, "cs_payload (reply slots, downstream OOB target)"),
48	    (0x076F, 0x11, "at_values (active test value slots)"),
49	    (0x07A0, 0x04, "cs_streamlist"),
50	    (0x07AC, 0x0B, "at_req (active test request flags)"),
51	    (0x07B7, 0x0C, "at_active (active test active flags)"),
52	    (0x07C3, 0x14, "c9_hidden_addr[0..9] (10 entries × 2 bytes)"),
53	    (0x07D7, 0x0A, "c9_hidden_idx[0..9] (10 entries × 1 byte)"),
54	    (0x07E1, 0x07, "c9_hidden_n / reply cursor / forward state"),
55	    (0x0810, 0x10, "dtc_seen (redirect target area)"),
56	]
57	
58	# Flat list of all addresses to read, and annotations for individual bytes.
59	ANNOTATIONS = {
60	    0x0740: "cs_payload[0]",
61	    0x0767: "cs_payload[39] (end)",
62	    0x076F: "at_values[0]",
63	    0x07A0: "cs_streamlist",
64	    0x07AC: "at_req[0]",
65	    0x07B7: "at_active[0]",
66	    0x07C3: "c9_hidden_addr[0] lo",
67	    0x07C4: "c9_hidden_addr[0] hi",
68	    0x07D7: "c9_hidden_idx[0]",
69	    0x07E0: "c9_hidden_idx[9] (last in-bounds)",
70	    0x07E1: "c9_hidden_n ← OVERWRITTEN at i=15 (lo)",
71	    0x07E2: "reply cursor ← OVERWRITTEN at i=15 (hi)",
72	    0x07E3: "forward flag",
73	    0x07E4: "forward state",
74	    0x07E5: "forward state",
75	    0x07E6: "forward state",
76	    0x07E7: "c9_sub_value",
77	    0x0810: "dtc_seen[0]",
78	}
79	
80	
81	def _build_addrs():
82	    """Build the sorted flat list of addresses to read from DUMP_REGIONS."""
83	    addrs = set()
84	    for start, length, _ in DUMP_REGIONS:
85	        addrs.update(range(start, start + length))
86	    return sorted(addrs)
87	
88	ALL_ADDRS = _build_addrs()

91	def simulate(addrs):
92	    """Dry-run the c9_find_hidden loop in Python and return the expected state.
93	
94	    Models the exact instruction sequence from $543F to $54C6, including the
95	    m-flag widths (CLM/SEM) confirmed in the disassembly.
96	    """
97	    ram = {}
98	
99	    def rb(addr):
100	        return ram.get(addr, 0)
101	
102	    def wb(addr, val):
103	        ram[addr] = val & 0xFF
104	
105	    def rw(addr):
106	        return rb(addr) | (rb(addr + 1) << 8)
107	
108	    def ww(addr, val):
109	        wb(addr, val & 0xFF)
110	        wb(addr + 1, (val >> 8) & 0xFF)
111	
112	    cs_argc = len(addrs) * 2
113	    for i, a in enumerate(addrs):
114	        ww(0x08D2 + 2 * i, a)
115	
116	    wb(HIDDEN_N, 0)
117	    old = rb(0x07E3)
118	    wb(0x07E3, old & ~0x03)
119	
120	    events = []
121	
122	    # The real loop is do-while: body runs, THEN 2*(i+1) < cs_argc is checked.
123	    for i in range(20):
124	        addr_val = rw(0x08D2 + 2 * i)
125	        is_hidden = FORWARD_LO <= addr_val < 0x6240
126	        if not is_hidden:
127	            pass
128	        else:
129	            wb(0x07E3, rb(0x07E3) | 0x01)
130	            n = rb(HIDDEN_N)
131	            addr_idx = (2 * n) & 0xFF
132	            addr_dest = HIDDEN_ADDR_BASE + addr_idx
133	            stored_word = addr_val - 0x5000
134	            ww(addr_dest, stored_word)
135	            events.append(f"  i={i:2d}  n={n:3d}  addr_store: [{addr_dest:04X}] <- "
136	                          f"${stored_word:04X}  (16-bit)")
137	            n_reread = rw(HIDDEN_N) & 0xFF
138	            idx_dest = HIDDEN_IDX_BASE + n_reread
139	            wb(idx_dest, i)
140	            events.append(f"         idx_store:  [{idx_dest:04X}] <- ${i:02X}  "
141	                          f"(8-bit, n_reread=${n_reread:02X})")
142	            wb(HIDDEN_N, (rb(HIDDEN_N) + 1) & 0xFF)
143	
144	        if 2 * (i + 1) >= cs_argc:
145	            break
146	
147	    return ram, events
148	
149	
150	def hex_dump(data, base, width=16):
151	    """Format bytes as a hex dump with address labels."""
152	    lines = []
153	    for off in range(0, len(data), width):
154	        row = data[off:off + width]
155	        hex_part = " ".join(f"{b:02X}" for b in row)
156	        lines.append(f"  ${base + off:04X}: {hex_part}")
157	    return "\n".join(lines)

159	
160	def annotated_diff(before, after, addrs, sim_ram, addr_trigger):
161	    """Print a before/after/expected table for every byte that changed, with labels."""
162	    word = addr_trigger - 0x5000
163	    lo, hi = word & 0xFF, (word >> 8) & 0xFF
164	    idx_dest = HIDDEN_IDX_BASE + lo
165	
166	    # Dynamic annotations based on the trigger address
167	    dyn = dict(ANNOTATIONS)
168	    dyn[idx_dest] = dyn.get(idx_dest, "") + f" ← REDIRECT TARGET ($07D7+${lo:02X})"
169	
170	    lines = []
171	    lines.append(f"  {'addr':>6s}  {'before':>6s}  {'after':>6s}  {'expect':>6s}  "
172	                 f"{'':1s}  description")
173	    lines.append(f"  {'─' * 6}  {'─' * 6}  {'─' * 6}  {'─' * 6}  ─  {'─' * 40}")
174	
175	    n_changed = 0
176	    for i, a in enumerate(addrs):
177	        b, af = before[i], after[i]
178	        exp = sim_ram.get(a)
179	        changed = b != af
180	
181	        if not changed:
182	            continue
183	        n_changed += 1
184	
185	        exp_str = f"${exp:02X}" if exp is not None else "  "
186	        match = "✓" if exp is not None and af == exp else "~" if exp is not None else " "
187	        label = dyn.get(a, "")
188	        lines.append(f"  ${a:04X}   ${b:02X}     ${af:02X}     {exp_str:>4s}  {match}  {label}")
189	
190	    return n_changed, "\n".join(lines)
191	
192	
193	def region_dump(data, addrs, title, sim_ram=None):
194	    """Print a labeled hex dump for each DUMP_REGION."""
195	    addr_to_val = dict(zip(addrs, data))
196	    print(f"\n  {title}")
197	    for start, length, label in DUMP_REGIONS:
198	        row_addrs = list(range(start, start + length))
199	        vals = [addr_to_val.get(a) for a in row_addrs]
200	        # Format in rows of 16
201	        for row_off in range(0, len(row_addrs), 16):
202	            chunk_addrs = row_addrs[row_off:row_off + 16]
203	            chunk_vals = vals[row_off:row_off + 16]
204	            cells = []
205	            for a, v in zip(chunk_addrs, chunk_vals):
206	                if v is None:
207	                    cells.append("..")
208	                elif sim_ram and a in sim_ram and v != sim_ram[a]:
209	                    cells.append(f"\033[1;33m{v:02X}\033[0m")  # yellow = unexpected
210	                elif sim_ram and a in sim_ram and v == sim_ram[a]:
211	                    cells.append(f"\033[1;32m{v:02X}\033[0m")  # green = matches model
212	                else:
213	                    cells.append(f"{v:02X}")
214	            addr_label = f"${chunk_addrs[0]:04X}"
215	            region_tag = f"  ; {label}" if row_off == 0 else ""
216	            print(f"    {addr_label}: {' '.join(cells)}{region_tag}")
217	
218	
219	def run_dry(addr):
220	    """Simulate and print the expected corruption."""
221	    addrs = [addr] * 16
222	    word = addr - 0x5000
223	    lo, hi = word & 0xFF, (word >> 8) & 0xFF
224	
225	    print(f"=== Dry run: 16 × C9 ${addr:04X} ===")
226	    print(f"Stored word: ${word:04X} (lo=${lo:02X} hi=${hi:02X})")
227	    print()
228	
229	    ram, events = simulate(addrs)
230	    for e in events:
231	        print(e)
232	
233	    print()
234	    idx_dest = HIDDEN_IDX_BASE + lo
235	    n_final = ram.get(HIDDEN_N, 0)
236	    cursor = ram.get(REPLY_CURSOR, 0)
237	    idx_val = ram.get(idx_dest, None)
238	
239	    print(f"Key signatures:")
240	    print(f"  $07E1 (c9_hidden_n):  ${n_final:02X}  (= ${lo:02X}+1 after INC)")
241	    print(f"  $07E2 (reply cursor): ${cursor:02X}  (= high byte of ${word:04X})")
242	    print(f"  ${idx_dest:04X} (idx redirect):  "
243	          f"{'$' + f'{idx_val:02X}' if idx_val is not None else 'unchanged'}  "
244	          f"(= loop index 15)")
245	    return ram
246	
247	
248	def read_addrs(c, addrs):
249	    """Read a list of addresses via C9 in chunks of 8."""
250	    result = bytearray()
251	    for i in range(0, len(addrs), 8):
252	        chunk = addrs[i:i + 8]
253	        result += c.read_memory(chunk)
254	    return bytes(result)
255	
256	
257	def run_live(dev, addr):
258	    """Run the overrun on the ECU and show full before/after RAM dumps."""
259	    trigger_addrs = [addr] * 16
260	    word = addr - 0x5000
261	    lo, hi = word & 0xFF, (word >> 8) & 0xFF
262	
263	    idx_dest = HIDDEN_IDX_BASE + lo
264	    expected_idx_val = 15
265	
266	    # Run the simulation for expected values
267	    sim_ram, _ = simulate(trigger_addrs)
268	
269	    os.makedirs(LOG_DIR, exist_ok=True)
270	    log = os.path.join(LOG_DIR, f"{datetime.date.today()}_c9-overrun-test.log")
271	    t = Transport(dev, log)
272	    t.note(f"c9_overrun_test addr=${addr:04X} x16")
273	
274	    try:
275	        c = Consult(t)
276	        c.ecu_ids = ENGINE_IDS
277	
278	        # --- Init ---
279	        r = c.init()
280	        if r is None:
281	            sys.exit("init failed (ignition on?)")
282	        attempt, secs, junk, ecu = r
283	        print(f"init ok: id {ecu:02X}, attempt {attempt}, {secs * 1000:.0f} ms")
284	
285	        # --- Before: read the full region ---
286	        print(f"\nReading {len(ALL_ADDRS)} bytes across the target region...")
287	        before = read_addrs(c, ALL_ADDRS)
288	        t.note(f"before ({len(before)} bytes)")
289	        region_dump(before, ALL_ADDRS, "BEFORE (baseline)")
290	
291	        # Check for stale corruption
292	        before_map = dict(zip(ALL_ADDRS, before))
293	        if before_map.get(idx_dest) == expected_idx_val:
294	            print(f"\n  ⚠  ${idx_dest:04X} already = ${expected_idx_val:02X} — "
295	                  f"stale corruption from a previous run.")
296	            print(f"      Key-cycle for a clean before/after, or continue to re-confirm.")
297	
298	        # --- Trigger ---
299	        print(f"\nSending trigger: 16 × C9 ${addr:04X} + F0...")
300	        payload = b"".join(bytes([0xC9, addr >> 8, addr & 0xFF])
301	                          for _ in range(16))
302	        c.command(payload)
303	        t.note("trigger sent, F0 accepted")
304	
305	        # Read one frame (c9_find_hidden already ran), then stop.
306	        frames = []
307	        try:
308	            f = c.read_frame(expected_len=16, timeout=1.0)
309	            frames.append(f)
310	        except TimeoutError:
311	            pass
312	        c.stop()
313	        t.note(f"stopped after {len(frames)} frame(s)")
314	        print(f"  read {len(frames)} frame(s), stopped")
315	
316	        # --- After: read the full region ---
317	        print(f"\nReading {len(ALL_ADDRS)} bytes again...")
318	        after = read_addrs(c, ALL_ADDRS)
319	        t.note(f"after ({len(after)} bytes)")
320	        region_dump(after, ALL_ADDRS, "AFTER (post-overrun)", sim_ram)
321	
322	        # --- Diff ---
323	        n_changed, diff_text = annotated_diff(before, after, ALL_ADDRS, sim_ram, addr)
324	        print(f"\n  CHANGED BYTES ({n_changed}):")
325	        if n_changed:
326	            print(diff_text)
327	        else:
328	            after_map = dict(zip(ALL_ADDRS, after))
329	            if after_map.get(idx_dest) == expected_idx_val:
330	                print("  (none — baseline was already corrupted from a previous run)")
331	            else:
332	                print("  (none)")
333	
334	        # --- Re-init and check persistence ---
335	        print(f"\nRe-init...")
336	        r = c.init()
337	        if r:
338	            post_reinit = read_addrs(c, ALL_ADDRS)
339	            persisted = sum(1 for b, a, p in zip(before, after, post_reinit)
340	                           if b != a and a == p)
341	            cleared = sum(1 for b, a, p in zip(before, after, post_reinit)
342	                          if b != a and a != p)
343	            if persisted:
344	                print(f"  {persisted} corrupted byte(s) PERSIST across re-init "
345	                      f"(only key cycle clears)")
346	            if cleared:
347	                print(f"  {cleared} corrupted byte(s) cleared by re-init")
348	            if not persisted and not cleared and n_changed == 0:
349	                print(f"  (no changes to track)")
350	        else:
351	            print(f"  re-init failed; turn key off and on")
352	
353	        # --- Verdict ---
354	        after_map = dict(zip(ALL_ADDRS, after))
355	        idx_hit = after_map.get(idx_dest) == expected_idx_val
356	
357	        print(f"\n{'=' * 60}")
358	        if idx_hit:
359	            print("BUG CONFIRMED: c9_find_hidden buffer overrun.")
360	            print(f"  ${idx_dest:04X} = ${expected_idx_val:02X}: "
361	                  f"idx store redirected to $07D7+${lo:02X} (n corrupted to ${lo:02X})")
362	            cursor_val = after_map.get(REPLY_CURSOR)
363	            if cursor_val == hi:
364	                print(f"  $07E2 = ${hi:02X}: reply cursor overwritten "
365	                      f"(high byte of ${word:04X})")
366	            elif cursor_val != before_map.get(REPLY_CURSOR):
367	                print(f"  $07E2 = ${cursor_val:02X}: changed (expected ${hi:02X}, "
368	                      f"reply builder ran once)")
369	        elif n_changed > 0:
370	            print("OVERRUN OCCURRED — bytes changed, check the diff above.")
371	        else:
372	            print("NO CHANGE — either baseline was already corrupted (key-cycle")
373	            print("and re-run) or the overrun didn't fire.")
374	        print(f"{'=' * 60}")
375	
376	    except (ConsultError, TimeoutError) as e:
377	        print(f"error: {e}")
378	    finally:
379	        t.close()
380	        print(f"\nlog: {log}")
381	
382	
383	def main():
384	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(
385	        description=__doc__,
386	        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
387	    ap.add_argument("--dev", default=DEFAULT_DEV,
388	                    help=f"serial device (default: {DEFAULT_DEV})")
389	    ap.add_argument("--addr", type=lambda x: int(x, 16), default=0x5D40,
390	                    help="C9 address to use, in hex (default: 5D40, must be in $5D40-$623F)")
391	    ap.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true",
392	                    help="simulate only, don't talk to the ECU")
393	    args = ap.parse_args()
394	
395	    if not FORWARD_LO <= args.addr <= FORWARD_HI:
396	        sys.exit(f"address ${args.addr:04X} is outside the forwarded range "
397	                 f"${FORWARD_LO:04X}-${FORWARD_HI:04X}")
398	
399	    print("c9_find_hidden buffer overrun test (ROM_ANALYSIS.md §10)")
400	    print(f"Target ECU: 23710-2L904 (1998 Maxima VQ30DE)")
401	    print(f"Test address: ${args.addr:04X} × 16")
402	    print()
403	
404	    # Always show the dry run first
405	    expected = run_dry(args.addr)
406	    print()
407	
408	    if args.dry_run:
409	        print("(dry run only, not talking to the ECU)")
410	        return
411	
412	    print("-" * 60)
413	    print("SAFETY: writes to Consult handler state only ($07C3-$08D6).")
414	    print("No code redirect possible (no RAM function pointers).")
415	    print("Corruption survives re-init — only key cycle clears it.")
416	    print("Run with key on, ENGINE OFF.")
417	    print("-" * 60)
418	    ok = input("Proceed? [y/N] ")
419	    if ok.strip().lower() != "y":
420	        sys.exit("not sent")
421	    print()
422	    run_live(args.dev, args.addr)
423	
424	
425	if __name__ == "__main__":
426	    main()
427
